`packages/ui/src/components/input.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ComponentChildren, JSX } from 'preact';
import { forwardRef } from 'preact/compat';
import { useRef } from 'preact/hooks';
import type { LucideIcon } from 'lucide-preact';
import { useAutosize } from '../lib/autosize.ts';
import { cx } from '../lib/cx.ts';
import { mergeRefs } from '../lib/refs.ts';
import { useFieldControl } from './field.tsx';

export interface InputProps extends Omit<JSX.InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement>, 'size' | 'icon' | 'prefix'> {
  size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';
  filled?: boolean;
  /** Иконка слева внутри поля (поиск). */
  icon?: LucideIcon;
  /** Неизменяемые части значения слева и справа: «https://», «.agent.app». */
  prefix?: string;
  suffix?: string;
  /** Подсказка-клавиша справа, например «/». */
  kbd?: string;
  /** Кнопки справа внутри поля: показать пароль, копировать, отправить. */
  action?: ComponentChildren;
}

export const Input = forwardRef<HTMLInputElement, InputProps>(function Input(
  { size = 'md', filled = false, icon: Icon, prefix, suffix, kbd, action, class: className, ...rest },
  ref,
) {
  const field = useFieldControl(rest);
  const input = (
    <input
      {...rest}
      {...field}
      ref={ref}
      class={cx('ui-input', size !== 'md' && `ui-input--${size}`, filled && 'ui-input--filled', className)}
    />
  );
  if (!Icon && !prefix && !suffix && !kbd && !action) return input;
  return (
    <div class="ui-input-group">
      {prefix && <span class="ui-input-group__addon">{prefix}</span>}
      {Icon && (
        <span class="ui-input-group__icon">
          <Icon aria-hidden="true" />
        </span>
      )}
      {input}
      {suffix && <span class="ui-input-group__addon">{suffix}</span>}
      {kbd && (
        <span class="ui-input-group__kbd">
          <kbd class="ui-kbd">{kbd}</kbd>
        </span>
      )}
      {action && <span class="ui-input-group__action">{action}</span>}
    </div>
  );
});

export interface TextareaProps extends JSX.TextareaHTMLAttributes<HTMLTextAreaElement> {
  /** Растягивать по содержимому. */
  autosize?: boolean;
}

export const Textarea = forwardRef<HTMLTextAreaElement, TextareaProps>(function Textarea(
  { autosize = false, class: className, value, ...rest },
  ref,
) {
  const own = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);
  const field = useFieldControl(rest);
  useAutosize(own, value, autosize);
  return <textarea {...rest} {...field} value={value} ref={mergeRefs(ref, own)} class={cx('ui-textarea', className)} />;
});

export interface SelectOption {
  value: string;
  label: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

export interface NativeSelectProps extends Omit<JSX.SelectHTMLAttributes<HTMLSelectElement>, 'size'> {
  options: readonly SelectOption[];
  size?: 'sm' | 'md';
  filled?: boolean;
}

/** Нативный `<select>`: для простых списков без описаний опций. */
export function NativeSelect({ options, size = 'md', filled = false, class: className, ...rest }: NativeSelectProps) {
  const field = useFieldControl(rest);
  return (
    <select
      {...rest}
      {...field}
      class={cx('ui-select', size === 'sm' && 'ui-select--sm', filled && 'ui-select--filled', className)}
    >
      {options.map(option => (
        <option key={option.value} value={option.value} disabled={option.disabled}>
          {option.label}
        </option>
      ))}
    </select>
  );
}
```
`packages/ui/src/components/choice.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ComponentChildren, JSX } from 'preact';
import { useLayoutEffect, useRef } from 'preact/hooks';
import type { LucideIcon } from 'lucide-preact';
import { cx } from '../lib/cx.ts';
import { useFieldControl } from './field.tsx';

type NativeInputProps = Omit<JSX.InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement>, 'type' | 'label' | 'size' | 'role'>;

export interface CheckboxProps extends NativeInputProps {
  label: ComponentChildren;
  description?: ComponentChildren;
  indeterminate?: boolean;
}

export function Checkbox({ label, description, indeterminate = false, class: className, ...rest }: CheckboxProps) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (ref.current) ref.current.indeterminate = indeterminate;
  }, [indeterminate]);
  return (
    <label class={cx('ui-check', className)}>
      <input {...rest} ref={ref} type="checkbox" class="ui-checkbox" />
      <span>
        {label}
        {description && <small>{description}</small>}
      </span>
    </label>
  );
}

export interface SwitchProps extends Omit<NativeInputProps, 'checked' | 'onChange'> {
  checked: boolean;
  onCheckedChange(checked: boolean): void;
  label: string;
  /** Подпись только для скринридера: видимый заголовок стоит в строке настроек. */
  hideLabel?: boolean;
  size?: 'sm' | 'md';
}

/** Для настроек, которые применяются сразу (дизайн-система, «Выбор»). */
export function Switch({
  checked,
  onCheckedChange,
  label,
  hideLabel = false,
  size = 'md',
  class: className,
  ...rest
}: SwitchProps) {
  const input = (
    <input
      {...rest}
      type="checkbox"
      role="switch"
      checked={checked}
      aria-checked={checked}
      onChange={event => onCheckedChange(event.currentTarget.checked)}
      class={cx('ui-switch', size === 'sm' && 'ui-switch--sm', hideLabel && className)}
      aria-label={hideLabel ? label : undefined}
    />
  );
  if (hideLabel) return input;
  return (
    <label class={cx('ui-check', className)}>
      {input}
      <span>{label}</span>
    </label>
  );
}

export interface RadioOption<V extends string> {
  value: V;
  label: ComponentChildren;
  description?: ComponentChildren;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

export interface RadioGroupProps<V extends string> {
  label: string;
  name: string;
  /** Тип значений выводится из списка вариантов. */
  value: NoInfer<V>;
  onChange(value: NoInfer<V>): void;
  options: ReadonlyArray<RadioOption<V>>;
}

/** Нативные радиокнопки: стрелки и Tab работают без JS. */
export function RadioGroup<V extends string>({ label, name, value, onChange, options }: RadioGroupProps<V>) {
  return (
    <div class="ui-radio-group" role="radiogroup" aria-label={label}>
      {options.map(option => (
        <label key={option.value} class="ui-check">
          <input
            type="radio"
            class="ui-radio"
            name={name}
            value={option.value}
            checked={option.value === value}
            disabled={option.disabled}
            onChange={() => onChange(option.value)}
          />
          <span>
            {option.label}
            {option.description && <small>{option.description}</small>}
          </span>
        </label>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export interface SliderProps extends Omit<NativeInputProps, 'value' | 'min' | 'max' | 'step' | 'onInput' | 'onChange'> {
  value: number;
  min?: number;
  max?: number;
  step?: number;
  onValueChange(value: number): void;
  /** Текст для скринридера вместо числа, например «6,000 credits». */
  formatValue?(value: number): string;
}

export function Slider({
  value,
  min = 0,
  max = 100,
  step = 1,
  onValueChange,
  formatValue,
  class: className,
  ...rest
}: SliderProps) {
  const field = useFieldControl(rest);
  const percent = max > min ? ((value - min) / (max - min)) * 100 : 0;
  return (
    <input
      {...rest}
      {...field}
      type="range"
      class={cx('ui-range', className)}
      min={min}
      max={max}
      step={step}
      value={value}
      style={{ '--val': `${percent}%` }}
      aria-valuetext={formatValue?.(value)}
      onInput={event => onValueChange(Number(event.currentTarget.value))}
    />
  );
}

export interface SegmentedOption<V extends string> {
  value: V;
  label?: ComponentChildren;
  icon?: LucideIcon;
  /** Обязателен, если у сегмента только иконка. */
  ariaLabel?: string;
}

export interface SegmentedControlProps<V extends string> {
  label: string;
  /** Тип значений выводится из списка вариантов. */
  value: NoInfer<V>;
  onChange(value: NoInfer<V>): void;
  options: ReadonlyArray<SegmentedOption<V>>;
  block?: boolean;
}

export function SegmentedControl<V extends string>({
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
  options,
  block = false,
}: SegmentedControlProps<V>) {
  return (
    <div class={cx('ui-segmented', block && 'ui-segmented--block')} role="group" aria-label={label}>
      {options.map(({ value: optionValue, label: text, icon: Icon, ariaLabel }) => (
        <button
          key={optionValue}
          type="button"
          aria-pressed={optionValue === value}
          aria-label={ariaLabel}
          onClick={() => onChange(optionValue)}
        >
          {Icon && <Icon aria-hidden="true" />}
          {text}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export interface ChoiceOption<V extends string> {
  value: V;
  label: ComponentChildren;
  icon?: LucideIcon;
}

export interface ChoiceGroupProps<V extends string> {
  label: string;
  name: string;
  /** Тип значений выводится из списка вариантов. */
  value: NoInfer<V>;
  onChange(value: NoInfer<V>): void;
  options: ReadonlyArray<ChoiceOption<V>>;
  /** Иконка слева от текста, а не над ним. */
  row?: boolean;
}

/** Карточки выбора (тема, режим, тариф) на нативных радиокнопках. */
export function ChoiceGroup<V extends string>({
  label,
  name,
  value,
  onChange,
  options,
  row = false,
}: ChoiceGroupProps<V>) {
  return (
    <div class="ui-choice-group" role="radiogroup" aria-label={label}>
      {options.map(({ value: optionValue, label: text, icon: Icon }) => (
        <label key={optionValue} class={cx('ui-choice', row && 'ui-choice--row')}>
          <input
            type="radio"
            name={name}
            value={optionValue}
            checked={optionValue === value}
            onChange={() => onChange(optionValue)}
          />
          {Icon && <Icon aria-hidden="true" class="ui-icon-lg" />}
          {text}
        </label>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```ts
export * from './components/field.tsx';
```

```ts
export type Orientation = 'horizontal' | 'vertical';
export function nextIndex(key: string, current: number, count: number, isDisabled: (index: number) => boolean, orientation: Orientation): number | null;

export function Tabs<V extends string>(props: { label: string; value: NoInfer<V>; onChange(value: NoInfer<V>): void; items: ReadonlyArray<TabItem<V>>; variant?: 'underline' | 'pills' }): JSX.Element;
```

```tsx
  expect(nextIndex('ArrowDown', 0, 3, i => i === 1, 'vertical')).toBe(2);
  expect(nextIndex('End', 0, 3, i => i === 2, 'vertical')).toBe(1);

// fireEvent.drag*/drop в happy-dom создаёт событие, которое не доходит до слушателей: шлём Event сами.
function drag(target: Element, type: string, files: File[] = []) {
  const event = new Event(type, { bubbles: true, cancelable: true });
  Object.defineProperty(event, 'dataTransfer', { value: { files } });
  act(() => {
    target.dispatchEvent(event);
  });
}

test('Dropzone highlights while dragging and hands over dropped files', () => {

  render(<Dropzone onFiles={onFiles} hint="CSV, XLSX, PDF · up to 50 MB" />);
  const zone = screen.getByText('browse').closest('label')!;
  drag(zone, 'dragenter');
  expect(zone.className).toBe('ui-dropzone is-dragover');
  const file = new File(['a,b'], 'data.csv', { type: 'text/csv' });
  drag(zone, 'drop', [file]);
  expect(zone.className).toBe('ui-dropzone');
  expect(onFiles).toHaveBeenCalledWith([file]);
});

});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Навигация с клавиатуры и вкладки**

`packages/ui/src/lib/roving.ts`:
```ts
export type Orientation = 'horizontal' | 'vertical';

/**
 * Индекс элемента, на который переходит фокус по клавише (WAI-ARIA: стрелки по кругу, Home, End),
 * пропуская недоступные. `null` — клавиша не навигационная или доступных элементов нет.
 */
export function nextIndex(
  key: string,
  current: number,
  count: number,
  isDisabled: (index: number) => boolean,
  orientation: Orientation,
): number | null {
  const forward = orientation === 'horizontal' ? 'ArrowRight' : 'ArrowDown';
  const backward = orientation === 'horizontal' ? 'ArrowLeft' : 'ArrowUp';
  let start: number;
  let step: number;
  if (key === forward) [start, step] = [current + 1, 1];
  else if (key === backward) [start, step] = [current - 1, -1];
  else if (key === 'Home') [start, step] = [0, 1];
  else if (key === 'End') [start, step] = [count - 1, -1];
  else return null;
  for (let n = 0; n < count; n++) {
    const index = (((start + step * n) % count) + count) % count;
    if (!isDisabled(index)) return index;
  }
  return null;
}
```
`packages/ui/src/components/tabs.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ComponentChildren } from 'preact';
import { useId, useRef } from 'preact/hooks';
import { cx } from '../lib/cx.ts';
import { nextIndex } from '../lib/roving.ts';

export interface TabItem<V extends string> {
  value: V;
  label: ComponentChildren;
  /** Счётчик-бейдж после подписи. */
  count?: number;
  disabled?: boolean;
  /** Содержимое вкладки. Без панелей Tabs работает как фильтр (вкладки-пилюли). */
  panel?: ComponentChildren;
}

export interface TabsProps<V extends string> {
  label: string;
  /** Тип значений выводится из списка вариантов. */
  value: NoInfer<V>;
  onChange(value: NoInfer<V>): void;
  items: ReadonlyArray<TabItem<V>>;
  variant?: 'underline' | 'pills';
}

/** WAI-ARIA Tabs: roving tabindex, стрелки по кругу, Home/End; выбор следует за фокусом. */
export function Tabs<V extends string>({ label, value, onChange, items, variant = 'underline' }: TabsProps<V>) {
  const base = useId();
  const tabs = useRef<Array<HTMLButtonElement | null>>([]);
  const hasPanels = items.some(item => item.panel !== undefined);
  const selected = items.findIndex(item => item.value === value);
  const focusable = selected >= 0 ? selected : items.findIndex(item => !item.disabled);
  const tabId = (index: number) => `${base}-tab-${index}`;
  const panelId = (index: number) => `${base}-panel-${index}`;

  const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent, index: number) => {
    const next = nextIndex(event.key, index, items.length, i => Boolean(items[i]!.disabled), 'horizontal');
    if (next === null) return;
    event.preventDefault();
    tabs.current[next]?.focus();
    onChange(items[next]!.value);
  };

  return (
    <>
      <div class={cx('ui-tabs', variant === 'pills' && 'ui-tabs--pills')} role="tablist" aria-label={label}>
        {items.map((item, index) => (
          <button
            key={item.value}
            ref={el => {
              tabs.current[index] = el;
            }}
            type="button"
            role="tab"
            id={tabId(index)}
            class="ui-tab"
            aria-selected={index === selected}
            aria-controls={hasPanels ? panelId(index) : undefined}
            tabIndex={index === focusable ? 0 : -1}
            disabled={item.disabled}
            onClick={() => onChange(item.value)}
            onKeyDown={event => onKeyDown(event, index)}
          >
            {item.label}
            {item.count !== undefined && <span class="ui-badge ui-badge--count">{item.count}</span>}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      {hasPanels &&
        items.map(
          (item, index) =>
            index === selected && (
              <div key={item.value} role="tabpanel" id={panelId(index)} aria-labelledby={tabId(index)} tabIndex={0}>
                {item.panel}
              </div>
            ),
        )}
    </>
  );
}
```
- [ ] **Step 3: Специальные поля**

`packages/ui/src/components/special-inputs.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Fragment, type ComponentChildren } from 'preact';
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { Minus, Plus, Upload, X } from 'lucide-preact';
import { cx } from '../lib/cx.ts';
import { IconButton } from './button.tsx';
import { Tag } from './chip.tsx';
import { useFieldControl } from './field.tsx';

export interface StepperProps {
  value: number;
  onChange(value: number): void;
  min?: number;
  max?: number;
  step?: number;
  /** Имя поля; внутри `<Field>` можно не задавать. */
  label?: string;
}

export function Stepper({ value, onChange, min = -Infinity, max = Infinity, step = 1, label }: StepperProps) {
  const field = useFieldControl({});
  const clamp = (n: number) => Math.min(max, Math.max(min, n));
  return (
    <div class="ui-stepper">
      <button
        type="button"
        aria-label={label ? `Decrease ${label}` : 'Decrease'}
        disabled={value <= min}
        onClick={() => onChange(clamp(value - step))}
      >
        <Minus aria-hidden="true" class="ui-icon-sm" />
      </button>
      <input
        {...field}
        type="number"
        inputMode="numeric"
        aria-label={label}
        value={value}
        min={Number.isFinite(min) ? min : undefined}
        max={Number.isFinite(max) ? max : undefined}
        step={step}
        onChange={event => {
          const typed = event.currentTarget.valueAsNumber;
          const next = Number.isFinite(typed) ? clamp(typed) : value;
          event.currentTarget.value = String(next);
          if (next !== value) onChange(next);
        }}
      />
      <button
        type="button"
        aria-label={label ? `Increase ${label}` : 'Increase'}
        disabled={value >= max}
        onClick={() => onChange(clamp(value + step))}
      >
        <Plus aria-hidden="true" class="ui-icon-sm" />
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

export interface OtpInputProps {
  /** Цифры кода без пропусков, длина 0…length. */
  value: string;
  onChange(value: string): void;
  onComplete?(value: string): void;
  length?: number;
  /** Разделитель «–» после каждых N ячеек. */
  groupSize?: number;
  label: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

/** Код подтверждения: автопереход между ячейками, Backspace назад, вставка всего кода. */
export function OtpInput({ value, onChange, onComplete, length = 6, groupSize = 3, label, disabled }: OtpInputProps) {
  const cells = useRef<Array<HTMLInputElement | null>>([]);
  const focus = (index: number) => cells.current[Math.max(0, Math.min(index, length - 1))]?.focus();

  const fill = (index: number, digits: string) => {
    const start = Math.min(index, value.length);
    const next = (value.slice(0, start) + digits + value.slice(start + digits.length)).slice(0, length);
    if (next !== value) onChange(next);
    focus(start + digits.length);
    if (next.length === length && digits.length > 0) onComplete?.(next);
    return next;
  };

  return (
    <div class="ui-otp" role="group" aria-label={label}>
      {Array.from({ length }, (_, index) => (
        <Fragment key={index}>
          {index > 0 && index % groupSize === 0 && (
            <span class="ui-otp__sep" aria-hidden="true">
              –
            </span>
          )}
          <input
            ref={el => {
              cells.current[index] = el;
            }}
            value={value[index] ?? ''}
            inputMode="numeric"
            autoComplete={index === 0 ? 'one-time-code' : 'off'}
            aria-label={`Digit ${index + 1} of ${length}`}
            disabled={disabled}
            onInput={event => {
              const el = event.currentTarget;
              const digits = el.value.replace(/\D/g, '');
              // В занятой ячейке остаётся последняя набранная цифра; в пустую может прийти весь код (автозаполнение).
              const typed = value[index] && digits.length > 1 ? digits.slice(-1) : digits;
              const next = typed ? fill(index, typed) : value.slice(0, index) + value.slice(index + 1);
              if (!typed && next !== value) onChange(next);
              el.value = next[index] ?? '';
            }}
            onKeyDown={event => {
              if (event.key === 'Backspace' && !value[index] && index > 0) {
                event.preventDefault();
                onChange(value.slice(0, index - 1));
                focus(index - 1);
              } else if (event.key === 'ArrowLeft') focus(index - 1);
              else if (event.key === 'ArrowRight') focus(index + 1);
            }}
            onPaste={event => {
              event.preventDefault();
              fill(index, (event.clipboardData?.getData('text') ?? '').replace(/\D/g, ''));
            }}
          />
        </Fragment>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export interface TagInputProps {
  values: readonly string[];
  onChange(values: string[]): void;
  /** Имя поля ввода; внутри `<Field>` можно не задавать. */
  label?: string;
  placeholder?: string;
  /** false — значение не добавляется (например, не email). */
  validate?(value: string): boolean;
}

/** Enter или запятая добавляют тег, Backspace в пустом поле удаляет последний. */
export function TagInput({ values, onChange, label, placeholder, validate }: TagInputProps) {
  const field = useFieldControl({});
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState('');
  const add = () => {
    const tag = draft.trim();
    if (!tag || values.includes(tag) || (validate && !validate(tag))) return;
    onChange([...values, tag]);
    setDraft('');
  };
  return (
    <div class="ui-tags">
      {values.map(tag => (
        <Tag key={tag} label={tag} onRemove={() => onChange(values.filter(v => v !== tag))} />
      ))}
      <input
        {...field}
        aria-label={label}
        value={draft}
        placeholder={placeholder}
        onInput={event => setDraft(event.currentTarget.value)}
        onKeyDown={event => {
          if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ',') {
            event.preventDefault();
            add();
          } else if (event.key === 'Backspace' && draft === '' && values.length > 0) {
            onChange(values.slice(0, -1));
          }
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

export interface DropzoneProps {
  onFiles(files: File[]): void;
  accept?: string;
  multiple?: boolean;
  title?: ComponentChildren;
  hint?: ComponentChildren;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

/** Зона загрузки: перетаскивание и выбор через нативный `<input type="file">` внутри `<label>`. */
export function Dropzone({ onFiles, accept, multiple = true, title, hint, disabled }: DropzoneProps) {
  const zone = useRef<HTMLLabelElement>(null);
  const [over, setOver] = useState(false);
  const take = (list: FileList | null | undefined) => {
    const files = [...(list ?? [])];
    if (files.length > 0) onFiles(multiple ? files : files.slice(0, 1));
  };
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = zone.current;
    if (!el || disabled) return;
    const enter = (event: DragEvent) => {
      event.preventDefault();
      setOver(true);
    };
    const leave = (event: DragEvent) => {
      event.preventDefault();
      setOver(false);
    };
    const drop = (event: DragEvent) => {
      leave(event);
      take(event.dataTransfer?.files);
    };
    // Обработчики через addEventListener: у <label> нет роли для перетаскивания в JSX-правилах a11y.
    el.addEventListener('dragenter', enter);
    el.addEventListener('dragover', enter);
    el.addEventListener('dragleave', leave);
    el.addEventListener('drop', drop);
    return () => {
      el.removeEventListener('dragenter', enter);
      el.removeEventListener('dragover', enter);
      el.removeEventListener('dragleave', leave);
      el.removeEventListener('drop', drop);
    };
  });
  return (
    <label ref={zone} class={cx('ui-dropzone', over && 'is-dragover')}>
      <span class="ui-dropzone__icon">
        <Upload aria-hidden="true" />
      </span>
      <b>
        {title ?? (
          <>
            Drop files here or <span class="ui-link">browse</span>
          </>
        )}
      </b>
      {hint && <span class="ui-hint">{hint}</span>}
      <input
        type="file"
        class="ui-sr-only"
        accept={accept}
        multiple={multiple}
        disabled={disabled}
        onChange={event => {
          take(event.currentTarget.files);
          event.currentTarget.value = '';
        }}
      />
    </label>
  );
}

const KEY_NAMES: Record<string, string> = {
  ' ': 'Space',
  ArrowUp: '↑',
  ArrowDown: '↓',
  ArrowLeft: '←',
  ArrowRight: '→',
  Enter: '↵',
  Backspace: '⌫',
  Delete: '⌦',
};
const MODIFIERS = new Set(['Meta', 'Control', 'Alt', 'Shift']);

/** Комбинация из события клавиатуры: `['⌘', '⇧', 'O']`; `null` — нажат только модификатор. */
export function shortcutFromEvent(event: KeyboardEvent): string[] | null {
  if (MODIFIERS.has(event.key)) return null;
  const key = KEY_NAMES[event.key] ?? (event.key.length === 1 ? event.key.toUpperCase() : event.key);
  return [event.metaKey && '⌘', event.ctrlKey && '⌃', event.altKey && '⌥', event.shiftKey && '⇧', key].filter(
    (part): part is string => Boolean(part),
  );
}

export interface ShortcutInputProps {
  value: readonly string[];
  onChange(keys: string[]): void;
  label: string;
  placeholder?: string;
}

/** Запись шортката: клик — запись (`aria-pressed`), следующая комбинация сохраняется, Esc — отмена. */
export function ShortcutInput({ value, onChange, label, placeholder = 'Not set' }: ShortcutInputProps) {
  const [recording, setRecording] = useState(false);
  return (
    <div class="ui-shortcut">
      <button
        type="button"
        class="ui-shortcut__keys"
        aria-pressed={recording}
        aria-label={recording ? `${label}: press a new shortcut` : `${label}: ${value.join(' ') || placeholder}`}
        onClick={() => setRecording(r => !r)}
        onBlur={() => setRecording(false)}
        onKeyDown={event => {
          if (!recording || event.key === 'Tab') return;
          event.preventDefault();
          if (event.key === 'Escape') return setRecording(false);
          const keys = shortcutFromEvent(event);
          if (!keys) return;
          onChange(keys);
          setRecording(false);
        }}
      >
        {recording ? (
          <span class="ui-shortcut__placeholder">Press keys…</span>
        ) : value.length > 0 ? (
          value.map((key, i) => (
            <kbd key={`${i}-${key}`} class="ui-kbd">
              {key}
            </kbd>
          ))
        ) : (
          <span class="ui-shortcut__placeholder">{placeholder}</span>
        )}
      </button>
      {value.length > 0 && (
        <IconButton
          icon={X}
          label={`Clear ${label}`}
          size="xs"
          class="ui-shortcut__clear"
          onClick={() => onChange([])}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { createContext, type ComponentChildren } from 'preact';
import { createPortal } from 'preact/compat';
import { useContext } from 'preact/hooks';

/**
 * Куда рендерить поповеры. По умолчанию — document.body; Dialog и Sheet подставляют свой
 * слой, чтобы меню внутри модалки не оказалось под её затемнением (z-index 40 < 50).
 */
export const PortalContext = createContext<HTMLElement | null>(null);

export function Portal({ children }: { children: ComponentChildren }) {
  const container = useContext(PortalContext);
  return createPortal(children, container ?? document.body);
}
```

`packages/ui/src/lib/presence.ts`:
```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { useReducedMotion } from './media.ts';

export type PresenceState = 'open' | 'closed';

/**
 * Слой с анимацией появления и исчезновения через `data-state`: при открытии первый кадр
 * рисуется в `closed`, затем `open` (CSS-переход играет); при закрытии слой живёт ещё `exitMs`.
 */
export function usePresence(open: boolean, exitMs: number): { mounted: boolean; state: PresenceState } {
  const reduced = useReducedMotion();
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(open);
  const [state, setState] = useState<PresenceState>(open ? 'open' : 'closed');
  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) {
      setMounted(true);
      const frame = requestAnimationFrame(() => setState('open'));
      return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
    }
    setState('closed');
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setMounted(false), reduced ? 0 : exitMs);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [open, exitMs, reduced]);
  return { mounted: open || mounted, state: open ? state : 'closed' };
}
```
`packages/ui/src/lib/dismiss.ts`:
```ts
import type { RefObject } from 'preact';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'preact/hooks';

export type DismissReason = 'escape' | 'outside';

// Открытые слои по порядку открытия: Esc и клик снаружи получает только верхний,
// поэтому Esc в меню внутри диалога закрывает меню, а не диалог.
const layers: object[] = [];

export interface DismissOptions {
  open: boolean;
  onDismiss(reason: DismissReason): void;
  /** Элементы слоя (сам слой, его триггер): нажатие в них не закрывает. */
  inside: ReadonlyArray<RefObject<HTMLElement>>;
  /** false — не закрывать по клику снаружи (alertdialog). */
  outsidePress?: boolean;
}

export function useDismiss({ open, onDismiss, inside, outsidePress = true }: DismissOptions): void {
  const [token] = useState(() => ({}));
  // Место в стеке — на всё время, пока слой открыт.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    layers.push(token);
    return () => {
      layers.splice(layers.indexOf(token), 1);
    };
  }, [open, token]);
  // Слушатели переподписываются на каждый рендер, чтобы видеть свежие onDismiss и inside.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const isTop = () => layers.at(-1) === token;
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key !== 'Escape' || !isTop() || event.defaultPrevented) return;
      event.preventDefault();
      onDismiss('escape');
    };
    const onPointerDown = (event: PointerEvent) => {
      if (!outsidePress || !isTop()) return;
      const target = event.target as Node | null;
      if (target && inside.some(ref => ref.current?.contains(target))) return;
      onDismiss('outside');
    };
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    document.addEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown, true);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
      document.removeEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown, true);
    };
  });
}
```
`packages/ui/src/lib/floating.ts`:
```ts
import type { RefObject } from 'preact';
import { useLayoutEffect } from 'preact/hooks';
import { autoUpdate, computePosition, flip, offset, shift, size, type Placement } from '@floating-ui/dom';

export type { Placement };

export interface FloatingOptions {
  open: boolean;
  anchor: RefObject<HTMLElement>;
  floating: RefObject<HTMLElement>;
  placement?: Placement;
  /** Отступ от якоря, px (в прототипе — 6). */
  gap?: number;
  /** Ширина как у якоря (кастомный select). */
  matchWidth?: boolean;
}

/**
 * Позиционирует слой рядом с якорем (Floating UI, strategy fixed): переворачивается у края
 * экрана, сдвигается внутрь, следит за прокруткой и размером. Координаты — inline-стилем,
 * фактическая сторона — в `data-placement` (от неё зависит transform-origin в CSS).
 */
export function useFloating({
  open,
  anchor,
  floating,
  placement = 'bottom-start',
  gap = 6,
  matchWidth = false,
}: FloatingOptions): void {
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const reference = anchor.current;
    const layer = floating.current;
    if (!open || !reference || !layer) return;
    const update = () => {
      void computePosition(reference, layer, {
        strategy: 'fixed',
        placement,
        middleware: [
          offset(gap),
          flip({ padding: 8 }),
          shift({ padding: 8 }),
          ...(matchWidth
            ? [
                size({
                  apply({ rects }) {
                    layer.style.width = `${rects.reference.width}px`;
                  },
                }),
              ]
            : []),
        ],
      }).then(({ x, y, placement: actual }) => {
        layer.style.left = `${x}px`;
        layer.style.top = `${y}px`;
        layer.dataset.placement = actual;
      });
    };
    return autoUpdate(reference, layer, update);
  }, [open, anchor, floating, placement, gap, matchWidth]);
}

/** Начальный стиль слоя до первого расчёта: вне потока и в углу, пока он прозрачен (data-state=closed). */
export const FLOATING_STYLE = { position: 'fixed', left: '0px', top: '0px' } as const;
```

`packages/ui/src/components/menu.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ComponentChildren, Ref } from 'preact';
import { useEffect, useId, useRef, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { Check, type LucideIcon } from 'lucide-preact';
import { cx } from '../lib/cx.ts';
import { useDismiss } from '../lib/dismiss.ts';
import { FLOATING_STYLE, useFloating, type Placement } from '../lib/floating.ts';
import { Portal } from '../lib/portal.tsx';
import { usePresence } from '../lib/presence.ts';
import { nextIndex } from '../lib/roving.ts';
import { DURATION_MS } from '../tokens.ts';
import { KbdCombo } from './display.tsx';

export type MenuEntry =
  | {
      kind: 'item';
      id: string;
      label: string;
      icon?: LucideIcon;
      /** Клавиши справа, например `['⌘', '⌫']`. */
      shortcut?: readonly string[];
      /** Другое содержимое справа (бейдж, стрелка подменю). */
      trail?: ComponentChildren;
      danger?: boolean;
      disabled?: boolean;
      onSelect(): void;
    }
  | {
      kind: 'checkbox';
      id: string;
      label: string;
      icon?: LucideIcon;
      checked: boolean;
      disabled?: boolean;
      onCheckedChange(checked: boolean): void;
    }
  | { kind: 'label'; id: string; label: string }
  | { kind: 'separator'; id: string };

export interface MenuTriggerProps {
  ref: Ref<HTMLButtonElement>;
  id: string;
  'aria-haspopup': 'menu';
  'aria-expanded': boolean;
  'aria-controls': string | undefined;
  onClick(): void;
  onKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent): void;
}

export interface MenuProps {
  /** Кнопка-триггер: пропсы нужно передать кнопке целиком (`<Button {...props}>`). */
  trigger(props: MenuTriggerProps): ComponentChildren;
  items: readonly MenuEntry[];
  placement?: Placement;
  width?: number;
}

const ITEM_SELECTOR = '[role="menuitem"], [role="menuitemcheckbox"]';
const isDisabled = (el: Element) => el.getAttribute('aria-disabled') === 'true';

/**
 * WAI-ARIA Menu Button: Enter, Space и ↓ открывают меню с фокусом на первом пункте, ↑ — на последнем;
 * стрелки по кругу, Home/End; Esc и выбор возвращают фокус на кнопку; Tab закрывает меню.
 */
export function Menu({ trigger, items, placement = 'bottom-start', width }: MenuProps) {
  const base = useId();
  const triggerId = `${base}-trigger`;
  const menuId = `${base}-menu`;
  const triggerRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [focusLast, setFocusLast] = useState(false);
  const { mounted, state } = usePresence(open, DURATION_MS.fast);
  useFloating({ open, anchor: triggerRef, floating: menuRef, placement });

  const close = (restoreFocus: boolean) => {
    setOpen(false);
    if (restoreFocus) triggerRef.current?.focus();
  };
  useDismiss({ open, onDismiss: reason => close(reason === 'escape'), inside: [triggerRef, menuRef] });

  const menuItems = () => [...(menuRef.current?.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(ITEM_SELECTOR) ?? [])];
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const enabled = menuItems().filter(el => !isDisabled(el));
    (focusLast ? enabled.at(-1) : enabled[0])?.focus();
  }, [open, focusLast]);

  const openMenu = (last: boolean) => {
    setFocusLast(last);
    setOpen(true);
  };

  const onMenuKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (event.key === 'Tab') {
      event.preventDefault();
      close(true);
      return;
    }
    const all = menuItems();
    const next = nextIndex(
      event.key,
      all.indexOf(document.activeElement as HTMLElement),
      all.length,
      i => isDisabled(all[i]!),
      'vertical',
    );
    if (next === null) return;
    event.preventDefault();
    all[next]!.focus();
  };

  const choose = (entry: Extract<MenuEntry, { kind: 'item' | 'checkbox' }>) => {
    if (entry.disabled) return;
    if (entry.kind === 'checkbox') return entry.onCheckedChange(!entry.checked);
    close(true);
    entry.onSelect();
  };

  return (
    <>
      {trigger({
        ref: triggerRef,
        id: triggerId,
        'aria-haspopup': 'menu',
        'aria-expanded': open,
        'aria-controls': mounted ? menuId : undefined,
        onClick: () => (open ? close(false) : openMenu(false)),
        onKeyDown: event => {
          if (open) return;
          if (event.key === 'ArrowDown' || event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
            event.preventDefault();
            openMenu(false);
          } else if (event.key === 'ArrowUp') {
            event.preventDefault();
            openMenu(true);
          }
        },
      })}
      {mounted && (
        <Portal>
          <div
            ref={menuRef}
            id={menuId}
            role="menu"
            aria-labelledby={triggerId}
            tabIndex={-1}
            class="ui-floating ui-menu"
            data-state={state}
            style={{ ...FLOATING_STYLE, width }}
            onKeyDown={onMenuKeyDown}
          >
            {items.map(entry => {
              if (entry.kind === 'label')
                return (
                  <div key={entry.id} class="ui-menu__label" role="presentation">
                    {entry.label}
                  </div>
                );
              if (entry.kind === 'separator') return <div key={entry.id} class="ui-menu__sep" role="none" />;
              const Icon = entry.icon;
              return (
                <button
                  key={entry.id}
                  type="button"
                  role={entry.kind === 'checkbox' ? 'menuitemcheckbox' : 'menuitem'}
                  aria-checked={entry.kind === 'checkbox' ? entry.checked : undefined}
                  aria-disabled={entry.disabled || undefined}
                  tabIndex={-1}
                  class={cx('ui-menu__item', entry.kind === 'item' && entry.danger && 'ui-menu__item--danger')}
                  onClick={() => choose(entry)}
                >
                  {Icon && <Icon aria-hidden="true" />}
                  {entry.label}
                  {entry.kind === 'checkbox' ? (
                    <span class="ui-menu__trail ui-menu__check">
                      <Check aria-hidden="true" />
                    </span>
                  ) : (
                    (entry.shortcut || entry.trail) && (
                      <span class="ui-menu__trail">
                        {entry.shortcut ? <KbdCombo keys={entry.shortcut} /> : entry.trail}
                      </span>
                    )
                  )}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </Portal>
      )}
    </>
  );
}
```
`packages/ui/src/components/select.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ComponentChildren } from 'preact';
import { useEffect, useId, useRef, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { Check, ChevronDown, type LucideIcon } from 'lucide-preact';
import { cx } from '../lib/cx.ts';
import { useDismiss } from '../lib/dismiss.ts';
import { FLOATING_STYLE, useFloating } from '../lib/floating.ts';
import { Portal } from '../lib/portal.tsx';
import { usePresence } from '../lib/presence.ts';
import { nextIndex } from '../lib/roving.ts';
import { DURATION_MS } from '../tokens.ts';
import { useFieldControl } from './field.tsx';

export interface SelectItem<V extends string> {
  value: V;
  label: string;
  /** Пояснение справа, например «Best plans». */
  description?: ComponentChildren;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

export interface SelectProps<V extends string> {
  /** Тип значений выводится из options; `undefined` — ничего не выбрано (показывается placeholder). */
  value: NoInfer<V> | undefined;
  onChange(value: NoInfer<V>): void;
  options: ReadonlyArray<SelectItem<V>>;
  placeholder?: string;
  /** Имя для скринридера, если select не внутри `<Field>`. */
  label?: string;
  icon?: LucideIcon;
  size?: 'sm' | 'md';
  disabled?: boolean;
}

/**
 * Кастомный select по шаблону WAI-ARIA «select-only combobox»: фокус всегда на кнопке,
 * активная опция — через aria-activedescendant. ↓/↑/Enter/Space открывают, стрелки и Home/End
 * двигают, Enter/Space выбирают, Esc и клик снаружи закрывают без выбора.
 */
export function Select<V extends string>({
  value,
  onChange,
  options,
  placeholder = 'Select…',
  label,
  icon: Icon,
  size = 'md',
  disabled,
}: SelectProps<V>) {
  const base = useId();
  const listId = `${base}-listbox`;
  const optionId = (index: number) => `${base}-option-${index}`;
  const field = useFieldControl({});
  const triggerRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const listRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [active, setActive] = useState(-1);
  const { mounted, state } = usePresence(open, DURATION_MS.fast);
  useFloating({ open, anchor: triggerRef, floating: listRef, matchWidth: true });
  useDismiss({
    open,
    onDismiss: () => setOpen(false),
    inside: [triggerRef, listRef],
  });

  const selectedIndex = options.findIndex(option => option.value === value);
  const isDisabled = (index: number) => Boolean(options[index]!.disabled);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (open && active >= 0)
      document.getElementById(`${base}-option-${active}`)?.scrollIntoView?.({ block: 'nearest' });
  }, [open, active, base]);

  const openList = () => {
    setActive(
      selectedIndex >= 0 ? selectedIndex : (nextIndex('Home', 0, options.length, isDisabled, 'vertical') ?? -1),
    );
    setOpen(true);
  };
  const choose = (index: number) => {
    const option = options[index];
    if (!option || option.disabled) return;
    setOpen(false);
    triggerRef.current?.focus();
    if (option.value !== value) onChange(option.value);
  };

  const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (!open) {
      if (['ArrowDown', 'ArrowUp', 'Enter', ' '].includes(event.key)) {
        event.preventDefault();
        openList();
      }
      return;
    }
    if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
      event.preventDefault();
      choose(active);
    } else if (event.key === 'Tab') {
      setOpen(false);
    } else {
      const next = nextIndex(event.key, active, options.length, isDisabled, 'vertical');
      if (next === null) return;
      event.preventDefault();
      setActive(next);
    }
  };

  const selected = options[selectedIndex];
  return (
    <>
      <button
        {...field}
        ref={triggerRef}
        type="button"
        role="combobox"
        class={cx('ui-input', size === 'sm' && 'ui-input--sm', 'ui-select-trigger')}
        data-placeholder={selected ? undefined : ''}
        aria-label={label}
        aria-haspopup="listbox"
        aria-expanded={open}
        aria-controls={listId}
        aria-activedescendant={open && active >= 0 ? optionId(active) : undefined}
        disabled={disabled}
        onClick={() => (open ? setOpen(false) : openList())}
        onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
      >
        {Icon && <Icon aria-hidden="true" class="ui-text-tertiary" />}
        <span>{selected?.label ?? placeholder}</span>
        <ChevronDown aria-hidden="true" />
      </button>
      {mounted && (
        <Portal>
          <div
            ref={listRef}
            id={listId}
            role="listbox"
            aria-labelledby={field.id}
            aria-label={field.id ? undefined : label}
            tabIndex={-1}
            class="ui-floating ui-menu"
            data-state={state}
            style={FLOATING_STYLE}
            // Фокус остаётся на кнопке; клик по опции ловится на списке (опции не фокусируются).
            onPointerDown={event => event.preventDefault()}
            onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
            onClick={event => {
              const option = (event.target as Element).closest<HTMLElement>('[role="option"]');
              if (option) choose(Number(option.dataset.index));
            }}
          >
            {options.map((option, index) => (
              <div
                key={option.value}
                id={optionId(index)}
                role="option"
                aria-selected={index === selectedIndex}
                aria-disabled={option.disabled || undefined}
                class="ui-menu__item"
                data-index={index}
                data-highlighted={index === active ? '' : undefined}
              >
                <span class="ui-menu__check">
                  <Check aria-hidden="true" />
                </span>
                {option.label}
                {option.description && <span class="ui-menu__trail">{option.description}</span>}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </Portal>
      )}
    </>
  );
}
```
`packages/ui/src/components/popover.tsx`:
```tsx
import { cloneElement, type ComponentChildren, type Ref, type VNode } from 'preact';
import { useEffect, useId, useRef, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { useDismiss } from '../lib/dismiss.ts';
import { FLOATING_STYLE, useFloating, type Placement } from '../lib/floating.ts';
import { Portal } from '../lib/portal.tsx';
import { usePresence } from '../lib/presence.ts';
import { mergeRefs } from '../lib/refs.ts';
import { DURATION_MS, TOOLTIP_DELAY_MS } from '../tokens.ts';

export interface PopoverTriggerProps {
  ref: Ref<HTMLButtonElement>;
  'aria-haspopup': 'dialog';
  'aria-expanded': boolean;
  'aria-controls': string | undefined;
  onClick(): void;
}

export interface PopoverProps {
  trigger(props: PopoverTriggerProps): ComponentChildren;
  /** Имя немодального диалога для скринридера. */
  label: string;
  placement?: Placement;
  children: ComponentChildren;
}

/** Немодальный поповер: фокус переходит внутрь, Esc возвращает его на кнопку, клик снаружи закрывает. */
export function Popover({ trigger, label, placement = 'bottom-start', children }: PopoverProps) {
  const panelId = `${useId()}-popover`;
  const triggerRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const panelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const { mounted, state } = usePresence(open, DURATION_MS.fast);
  useFloating({ open, anchor: triggerRef, floating: panelRef, placement });
  const close = (restoreFocus: boolean) => {
    setOpen(false);
    if (restoreFocus) triggerRef.current?.focus();
  };
  useDismiss({ open, onDismiss: reason => close(reason === 'escape'), inside: [triggerRef, panelRef] });
  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) panelRef.current?.focus();
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <>
      {trigger({
        ref: triggerRef,
        'aria-haspopup': 'dialog',
        'aria-expanded': open,
        'aria-controls': mounted ? panelId : undefined,
        onClick: () => (open ? close(false) : setOpen(true)),
      })}
      {mounted && (
        <Portal>
          <div
            ref={panelRef}
            id={panelId}
            role="dialog"
            aria-label={label}
            tabIndex={-1}
            class="ui-floating ui-popover"
            data-state={state}
            style={FLOATING_STYLE}
          >
            {children}
          </div>
        </Portal>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

export interface TooltipProps {
  content: string;
  side?: 'top' | 'right' | 'bottom' | 'left';
  delay?: number;
  /** Один элемент, который принимает ref (Button, IconButton или DOM-элемент). */
  children: VNode;
}

type Handler = ((event: Event) => void) | undefined;

/**
 * Тултип по наведению и фокусу с задержкой 400 мс; Esc скрывает его, не трогая фокус (WCAG 1.4.13).
 * Дублирует подпись, но не заменяет её: у иконочной кнопки всё равно должен быть aria-label.
 */
export function Tooltip({ content, side = 'top', delay = TOOLTIP_DELAY_MS, children }: TooltipProps) {
  const id = `${useId()}-tooltip`;
  const anchor = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
  const tip = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  // wanted — указатель или фокус на элементе; open — тултип показан (после задержки).
  const [wanted, setWanted] = useState(false);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  useFloating({ open, anchor, floating: tip, placement: side, gap: 8 });
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!wanted) return setOpen(false);
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setOpen(true), delay);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [wanted, delay]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') setOpen(false);
    };
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [open]);

  const own = children.props as Record<string, unknown>;
  const chain = (name: string, next: boolean) => (event: Event) => {
    (own[name] as Handler)?.(event);
    setWanted(next);
  };
  const describedBy = [own['aria-describedby'], open && content !== own['aria-label'] && id].filter(Boolean).join(' ');

  return (
    <>
      {cloneElement(children, {
        ref: mergeRefs(anchor, (children as VNode & { ref?: Ref<HTMLElement> }).ref),
        'aria-describedby': describedBy || undefined,
        onPointerEnter: chain('onPointerEnter', true),
        onPointerLeave: chain('onPointerLeave', false),
        onFocus: chain('onFocus', true),
        onBlur: chain('onBlur', false),
      })}
      {open && (
        <Portal>
          <div ref={tip} id={id} role="tooltip" class="ui-tooltip" data-side={side} style={FLOATING_STYLE}>
            {content}
          </div>
        </Portal>
      )}
    </>
  );
}
```

```ts
export function tabbables(root: HTMLElement): HTMLElement[];
export function useFocusTrap(container: RefObject<HTMLElement>, active: boolean): void; // [data-autofocus] → первый фокусируемый → контейнер; возврат фокуса
export function useScrollLock(active: boolean): void;
export interface HotkeyOptions { mod?: boolean; enabled?: boolean }
export function useHotkey(key: string, handler: (event: KeyboardEvent) => void, options?: HotkeyOptions): void;
export interface DialogProps {
  open: boolean; onOpenChange(open: boolean): void; title: ComponentChildren; description?: ComponentChildren;
  size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl' | 'full'; serifTitle?: boolean; role?: 'dialog' | 'alertdialog';
  icon?: ComponentChildren; footer?: ComponentChildren; children?: ComponentChildren;
}
export function Dialog(props: DialogProps): JSX.Element | null;
export function ConfirmDialog(props: { open: boolean; onOpenChange(open: boolean): void; title: ComponentChildren; description?: ComponentChildren; confirmLabel: string; cancelLabel?: string; onConfirm(): void; danger?: boolean; icon?: LucideIcon; busy?: boolean }): JSX.Element;
export function Sheet(props: { open: boolean; onOpenChange(open: boolean): void; title: ComponentChildren; footer?: ComponentChildren; children?: ComponentChildren }): JSX.Element | null;
export interface CommandItem { id: string; label: string; icon?: LucideIcon; shortcut?: readonly string[]; trail?: ComponentChildren; keywords?: string; onSelect(): void }
export interface CommandGroup { label: string; items: readonly CommandItem[] }
export function filterCommands(groups: readonly CommandGroup[], query: string): CommandGroup[];
export function CommandPalette(props: { open: boolean; onOpenChange(open: boolean): void; groups: readonly CommandGroup[]; label?: string; placeholder?: string; emptyText?: string }): JSX.Element | null;
```

`packages/ui/test/modal.test.tsx`:
```tsx
import { expect, mock, test } from 'bun:test';
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/preact';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { Settings, SquarePen } from 'lucide-preact';
import {
  Button,
  CommandPalette,
  ConfirmDialog,
  Dialog,
  filterCommands,
  Input,
  Select,
  Sheet,
  useHotkey,
  type CommandGroup,
} from '../src/index.ts';
import { gone } from './helpers.ts';

function KeyDialog() {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [expiry, setExpiry] = useState<'30' | '90' | undefined>('30');
  return (
    <>
      <Button onClick={() => setOpen(true)}>Create key</Button>
      <Dialog
        open={open}
        onOpenChange={setOpen}
        title="Create API key"
        description="Keys let your code start tasks."
        footer={<Button variant="primary">Create</Button>}
      >
        <Input aria-label="Name" />
        <Select
          label="Expires"
          value={expiry}
          onChange={setExpiry}
          options={[
            { value: '30', label: 'In 30 days' },
            { value: '90', label: 'In 90 days' },
          ]}
        />
      </Dialog>
    </>
  );
}

test('Dialog moves focus to the first field, traps Tab and returns focus on Escape', async () => {
  const user = userEvent.setup();
  render(<KeyDialog />);
  const opener = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create key' });
  await user.click(opener);
  const dialog = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Create API key' });
  expect(dialog.getAttribute('aria-modal')).toBe('true');
  expect(dialog.getAttribute('aria-describedby')).toBe(screen.getByText('Keys let your code start tasks.').id);
  expect(document.activeElement).toBe(screen.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Name' }));
  expect(document.body.style.overflow).toBe('hidden');
  await user.keyboard('{Shift>}{Tab}{/Shift}');
  expect(document.activeElement).toBe(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Close' }));
  await user.tab();
  expect(document.activeElement).toBe(screen.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Name' }));
  await user.keyboard('{Escape}');
  expect(document.activeElement).toBe(opener);
  expect(document.body.style.overflow).toBe('');
  await gone(() => screen.queryByRole('dialog'));
});

test('a click on the backdrop closes the dialog, a click inside does not', async () => {
  const user = userEvent.setup();
  render(<KeyDialog />);
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create key' }));
  const dialog = screen.getByRole('dialog');
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Name' }));
  expect(dialog.closest('.ui-overlay')?.getAttribute('data-state')).not.toBe('closed');
  fireEvent.pointerDown(dialog.closest('.ui-overlay')!);
  await gone(() => screen.queryByRole('dialog'));
});

test('a select inside a dialog renders its list in the dialog layer, and Escape closes only the list', async () => {
  const user = userEvent.setup();
  render(<KeyDialog />);
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create key' }));
  const combobox = screen.getByRole('combobox', { name: 'Expires' });
  await user.click(combobox);
  const listbox = screen.getByRole('listbox');
  expect(screen.getByRole('dialog').closest('.ui-overlay')!.contains(listbox)).toBe(true);
  await user.keyboard('{Escape}');
  expect(combobox.getAttribute('aria-expanded')).toBe('false');
  expect(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Create API key' })).toBeTruthy();
});

function DeleteTask({ onConfirm }: { onConfirm(): void }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(true);
  return (
    <ConfirmDialog
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={setOpen}
      title="Delete this task?"
      description="This can't be undone."
      confirmLabel="Delete task"
      onConfirm={onConfirm}
    />
  );
}

test('ConfirmDialog is an alertdialog focused on Cancel that ignores outside clicks', async () => {
  const user = userEvent.setup();
  const onConfirm = mock(() => {});
  render(<DeleteTask onConfirm={onConfirm} />);
  const dialog = await screen.findByRole('alertdialog', { name: 'Delete this task?' });
  expect(document.activeElement).toBe(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancel' }));
  expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Close' })).toBeNull();
  fireEvent.pointerDown(dialog.closest('.ui-overlay')!);
  expect(screen.getByRole('alertdialog')).toBeTruthy();
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Delete task' }));
  expect(onConfirm).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancel' }));
  await gone(() => screen.queryByRole('alertdialog'));
});

function TaskDetails() {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  return (
    <>
      <Button onClick={() => setOpen(true)}>Details</Button>
      <Sheet open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen} title="Task details">
        <p>Completed</p>
      </Sheet>
    </>
  );
}

test('Sheet is a modal dialog with a close button', async () => {
  const user = userEvent.setup();
  render(<TaskDetails />);
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Details' }));
  expect(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Task details' }).getAttribute('aria-modal')).toBe('true');
  expect(document.activeElement).toBe(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Close' }));
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Close' }));
  await gone(() => screen.queryByRole('dialog'));
  expect(document.activeElement).toBe(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Details' }));
});

const groups = (onNew: () => void, onSettings: () => void): CommandGroup[] => [
  {
    label: 'Actions',
    items: [
      { id: 'new', label: 'New task', icon: SquarePen, shortcut: ['⌘', 'O'], onSelect: onNew },
      { id: 'settings', label: 'Open settings', icon: Settings, keywords: 'preferences', onSelect: onSettings },
    ],
  },
  { label: 'Recent tasks', items: [{ id: 't1', label: 'Compare agent frameworks', onSelect: () => {} }] },
];

test('filterCommands matches labels and keywords and drops empty groups', () => {
  const result = filterCommands(
    groups(
      () => {},
      () => {},
    ),
    ' PREFER',
  );
  expect(result.map(g => [g.label, g.items.map(i => i.id)])).toEqual([['Actions', ['settings']]]);
});

function Palette({ onSettings }: { onSettings(): void }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  useHotkey('k', () => setOpen(true), { mod: true });
  return <CommandPalette open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen} groups={groups(() => {}, onSettings)} />;
}
```

`packages/ui/src/lib/focus.ts`:
```ts
import type { RefObject } from 'preact';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'preact/hooks';

const TABBABLE = [
  'a[href]',
  'button:not([disabled])',
  'input:not([disabled]):not([type="hidden"])',
  'select:not([disabled])',
  'textarea:not([disabled])',
  '[tabindex]',
  '[contenteditable="true"]',
].join(',');

/** Элементы, до которых доходит Tab, в порядке документа. */
export function tabbables(root: HTMLElement): HTMLElement[] {
  return [...root.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(TABBABLE)].filter(
    el => el.tabIndex >= 0 && !el.closest('[inert], [hidden], [aria-hidden="true"]'),
  );
}

// Активные ловушки по порядку включения: Tab обрабатывает только верхняя (диалог поверх диалога).
const traps: object[] = [];

/**
 * Пока `active`: фокус внутри контейнера — на `[data-autofocus]`, иначе на первом фокусируемом,
 * иначе на самом контейнере; Tab и Shift+Tab ходят по кругу. При выключении фокус возвращается
 * на элемент, который был в фокусе до включения.
 */
export function useFocusTrap(container: RefObject<HTMLElement>, active: boolean): void {
  const [token] = useState(() => ({}));
  useEffect(() => {
    const root = container.current;
    if (!active || !root) return;
    const previous = document.activeElement instanceof HTMLElement ? document.activeElement : null;
    traps.push(token);
    (root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-autofocus]') ?? tabbables(root)[0] ?? root).focus();
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key !== 'Tab' || traps.at(-1) !== token) return;
      const items = tabbables(root);
      const current = document.activeElement;
      const first = items[0];
      const last = items.at(-1);
      if (!first || !last) {
        event.preventDefault();
        root.focus();
      } else if (event.shiftKey && (current === first || !root.contains(current))) {
        event.preventDefault();
        last.focus();
      } else if (!event.shiftKey && (current === last || !root.contains(current))) {
        event.preventDefault();
        first.focus();
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => {
      traps.splice(traps.indexOf(token), 1);
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
      previous?.focus();
    };
  }, [active, container, token]);
}

let locks = 0;
let previousOverflow = '';

/** Блокирует прокрутку страницы под модальным слоем; вложенные блокировки считаются. */
export function useScrollLock(active: boolean): void {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!active) return;
    if (locks++ === 0) {
      previousOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    }
    return () => {
      if (--locks === 0) document.body.style.overflow = previousOverflow;
    };
  }, [active]);
}
```
`packages/ui/src/lib/hotkey.ts`:
```ts
import { useEffect } from 'preact/hooks';

export interface HotkeyOptions {
  /** ⌘ на macOS или Ctrl на остальных системах. */
  mod?: boolean;
  enabled?: boolean;
}

function isEditable(target: EventTarget | null): boolean {
  return (
    target instanceof HTMLElement &&
    (target.isContentEditable || ['INPUT', 'TEXTAREA', 'SELECT'].includes(target.tagName))
  );
}

/** Глобальная горячая клавиша. Без `mod` не срабатывает, пока пользователь печатает в поле. */
export function useHotkey(
  key: string,
  handler: (event: KeyboardEvent) => void,
  { mod = false, enabled = true }: HotkeyOptions = {},
): void {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return;
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key.toLowerCase() !== key.toLowerCase()) return;
      if (mod !== (event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey)) return;
      if (!mod && isEditable(event.target)) return;
      event.preventDefault();
      handler(event);
    };
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  });
}
```

`packages/ui/src/components/dialog.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ComponentChildren } from 'preact';
import { useId, useMemo, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { Trash2, X, type LucideIcon } from 'lucide-preact';
import { cx } from '../lib/cx.ts';
import { useDismiss } from '../lib/dismiss.ts';
import { useFocusTrap, useScrollLock } from '../lib/focus.ts';
import { Portal, PortalContext } from '../lib/portal.tsx';
import { usePresence } from '../lib/presence.ts';
import { DURATION_MS } from '../tokens.ts';
import { Button, IconButton } from './button.tsx';

/** Элемент-слой: state, а не ref — он нужен PortalContext при рендере детей. */
function useLayer() {
  const [element, setElement] = useState<HTMLElement | null>(null);
  const ref = useMemo(() => ({ current: element }), [element]);
  return [ref, setElement] as const;
}

export interface DialogProps {
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange(open: boolean): void;
  title: ComponentChildren;
  description?: ComponentChildren;
  size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl' | 'full';
  /** Заголовок шрифтом Instrument Serif (тарифы, промо). */
  serifTitle?: boolean;
  /** alertdialog — подтверждение: без крестика, клик снаружи не закрывает. */
  role?: 'dialog' | 'alertdialog';
  /** Над заголовком, например иконка опасного действия. */
  icon?: ComponentChildren;
  footer?: ComponentChildren;
  children?: ComponentChildren;
}

/**
 * Модальный диалог: фокус внутрь (элемент с `data-autofocus` или первый фокусируемый), Tab по кругу,
 * Esc и клик по затемнению закрывают, фокус возвращается на элемент, открывший диалог.
 */
export function Dialog({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  title,
  description,
  size = 'md',
  serifTitle = false,
  role = 'dialog',
  icon,
  footer,
  children,
}: DialogProps) {
  const base = useId();
  const titleId = `${base}-title`;
  const descriptionId = `${base}-description`;
  const [panel, setPanel] = useLayer();
  const [overlay, setOverlay] = useLayer();
  const { mounted, state } = usePresence(open, DURATION_MS.base);
  useFocusTrap(panel, open);
  useScrollLock(open);
  useDismiss({ open, onDismiss: () => onOpenChange(false), inside: [panel], outsidePress: role === 'dialog' });
  if (!mounted) return null;
  return (
    <Portal>
      <div ref={setOverlay} class="ui-overlay" data-state={state}>
        <PortalContext.Provider value={overlay.current}>
          <div
            ref={setPanel}
            role={role}
            aria-modal="true"
            aria-labelledby={titleId}
            aria-describedby={description ? descriptionId : undefined}
            tabIndex={-1}
            class={cx('ui-dialog', size !== 'md' && `ui-dialog--${size}`)}
          >
            <div class="ui-dialog__header">
              {icon}
              <h2 id={titleId} class={cx('ui-dialog__title', serifTitle && 'ui-dialog__title--serif')}>
                {title}
              </h2>
              {description && (
                <p id={descriptionId} class="ui-dialog__desc">
                  {description}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
            {children && <div class="ui-dialog__body">{children}</div>}
            {footer && <div class="ui-dialog__footer">{footer}</div>}
            {/* Крестик последним в DOM: Tab сначала проходит по содержимому. */}
            {role === 'dialog' && (
              <IconButton icon={X} label="Close" class="ui-dialog__close" onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)} />
            )}
          </div>
        </PortalContext.Provider>
      </div>
    </Portal>
  );
}

export interface ConfirmDialogProps {
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange(open: boolean): void;
  title: ComponentChildren;
  description?: ComponentChildren;
  confirmLabel: string;
  cancelLabel?: string;
  onConfirm(): void;
  /** Необратимое действие: красная кнопка и иконка (по умолчанию). */
  danger?: boolean;
  icon?: LucideIcon;
  /** Действие выполняется: кнопка подтверждения со спиннером. */
  busy?: boolean;
}

/** Подтверждение (alertdialog): фокус на «Cancel», деструктивная кнопка справа. */
export function ConfirmDialog({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  title,
  description,
  confirmLabel,
  cancelLabel = 'Cancel',
  onConfirm,
  danger = true,
  icon: Icon = Trash2,
  busy = false,
}: ConfirmDialogProps) {
  return (
    <Dialog
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={onOpenChange}
      role="alertdialog"
      size="sm"
      title={title}
      description={description}
      icon={
        danger && (
          <span class="ui-dialog__icon ui-dialog__icon--danger">
            <Icon aria-hidden="true" />
          </span>
        )
      }
      footer={
        <>
          <Button variant="secondary" data-autofocus onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}>
            {cancelLabel}
          </Button>
          <Button variant={danger ? 'danger' : 'primary'} loading={busy} onClick={onConfirm}>
            {confirmLabel}
          </Button>
        </>
      }
    />
  );
}

export interface SheetProps {
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange(open: boolean): void;
  title: ComponentChildren;
  footer?: ComponentChildren;
  children?: ComponentChildren;
}

/** Боковая панель справа: модальная, поведение как у Dialog. */
export function Sheet({ open, onOpenChange, title, footer, children }: SheetProps) {
  const titleId = `${useId()}-title`;
  const [panel, setPanel] = useLayer();
  const { mounted, state } = usePresence(open, DURATION_MS.base);
  useFocusTrap(panel, open);
  useScrollLock(open);
  useDismiss({ open, onDismiss: () => onOpenChange(false), inside: [panel] });
  if (!mounted) return null;
  return (
    <Portal>
      <div class="ui-overlay" data-state={state} aria-hidden="true" />
      <aside
        ref={setPanel}
        class="ui-sheet"
        data-state={state}
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby={titleId}
        tabIndex={-1}
      >
        <PortalContext.Provider value={panel.current}>
          <div class="ui-sheet__header">
            <b id={titleId}>{title}</b>
            <IconButton icon={X} label="Close" onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)} />
          </div>
          <div class="ui-sheet__body">{children}</div>
          {footer && <div class="ui-sheet__footer">{footer}</div>}
        </PortalContext.Provider>
      </aside>
    </Portal>
  );
}
```
`packages/ui/src/components/command.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ComponentChildren } from 'preact';
import { useEffect, useId, useMemo, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { Search, type LucideIcon } from 'lucide-preact';
import { useDismiss } from '../lib/dismiss.ts';
import { useFocusTrap, useScrollLock } from '../lib/focus.ts';
import { Portal } from '../lib/portal.tsx';
import { usePresence } from '../lib/presence.ts';
import { nextIndex } from '../lib/roving.ts';
import { DURATION_MS } from '../tokens.ts';
import { Kbd, KbdCombo } from './display.tsx';

export interface CommandItem {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  icon?: LucideIcon;
  shortcut?: readonly string[];
  trail?: ComponentChildren;
  /** Дополнительные слова для поиска. */
  keywords?: string;
  onSelect(): void;
}

export interface CommandGroup {
  label: string;
  items: readonly CommandItem[];
}

export interface CommandPaletteProps {
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange(open: boolean): void;
  groups: readonly CommandGroup[];
  label?: string;
  placeholder?: string;
  emptyText?: string;
}

export function filterCommands(groups: readonly CommandGroup[], query: string): CommandGroup[] {
  const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
  return groups
    .map(group => ({
      ...group,
      items: group.items.filter(item => `${item.label} ${item.keywords ?? ''}`.toLowerCase().includes(q)),
    }))
    .filter(group => group.items.length > 0);
}

/**
 * Палитра команд (⌘K открывает приложение через useHotkey): поле — combobox, список — listbox
 * с aria-activedescendant; ввод фильтрует, ↑/↓ двигают, Enter выполняет, Esc закрывает.
 */
export function CommandPalette({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  groups,
  label = 'Command palette',
  placeholder = 'Search tasks, files and commands…',
  emptyText = 'No results',
}: CommandPaletteProps) {
  const base = useId();
  const listId = `${base}-list`;
  const optionId = (id: string) => `${base}-option-${id}`;
  const [panel, setPanel] = useState<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const panelRef = useMemo(() => ({ current: panel }), [panel]);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [active, setActive] = useState(0);
  const { mounted, state } = usePresence(open, DURATION_MS.base);
  useFocusTrap(panelRef, open);
  useScrollLock(open);
  useDismiss({ open, onDismiss: () => onOpenChange(false), inside: [panelRef] });
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    setQuery('');
    setActive(0);
  }, [open]);

  const visible = filterCommands(groups, query);
  const flat = visible.flatMap(group => group.items);
  const current = flat[Math.min(active, flat.length - 1)];
  const run = (item: CommandItem | undefined) => {
    if (!item) return;
    onOpenChange(false);
    item.onSelect();
  };
  const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (event.key === 'Enter') {
      event.preventDefault();
      run(current);
      return;
    }
    if (event.key !== 'ArrowDown' && event.key !== 'ArrowUp') return;
    const next = nextIndex(event.key, flat.indexOf(current!), flat.length, () => false, 'vertical');
    if (next === null) return;
    event.preventDefault();
    setActive(next);
    document.getElementById(optionId(flat[next]!.id))?.scrollIntoView?.({ block: 'nearest' });
  };

  if (!mounted) return null;
  return (
    <Portal>
      <div class="ui-overlay" data-state={state}>
        <div ref={setPanel} class="ui-command" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label={label} tabIndex={-1}>
          <div class="ui-command__input">
            <Search aria-hidden="true" />
            <input
              data-autofocus
              role="combobox"
              aria-label={label}
              aria-expanded="true"
              aria-controls={listId}
              aria-autocomplete="list"
              aria-activedescendant={current ? optionId(current.id) : undefined}
              placeholder={placeholder}
              value={query}
              onInput={event => {
                setQuery(event.currentTarget.value);
                setActive(0);
              }}
              onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
            />
            <Kbd>esc</Kbd>
          </div>
          <div
            id={listId}
            role="listbox"
            aria-label={label}
            tabIndex={-1}
            class="ui-command__list"
            onPointerDown={event => event.preventDefault()}
            onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
            onClick={event => {
              const option = (event.target as Element).closest<HTMLElement>('[role="option"]');
              if (option) run(flat.find(item => optionId(item.id) === option.id));
            }}
          >
            {flat.length === 0 && <div class="ui-command__empty">{emptyText}</div>}
            {visible.map(group => (
              <div key={group.label} role="group" aria-labelledby={`${base}-group-${group.label}`}>
                <div id={`${base}-group-${group.label}`} class="ui-menu__label" role="presentation">
                  {group.label}
                </div>
                {group.items.map(item => {
                  const Icon = item.icon;
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={item.id}
                      id={optionId(item.id)}
                      role="option"
                      aria-selected={item === current}
                      class="ui-menu__item"
                      data-highlighted={item === current ? '' : undefined}
                    >
                      {Icon && <Icon aria-hidden="true" />}
                      {item.label}
                      {(item.shortcut || item.trail) && (
                        <span class="ui-menu__trail">
                          {item.shortcut ? <KbdCombo keys={item.shortcut} /> : item.trail}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
          <div class="ui-command__footer">
            <span>
              <Kbd>↑</Kbd>
              <Kbd>↓</Kbd>
              navigate
            </span>
            <span>
              <Kbd>↵</Kbd>
              open
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Portal>
  );
}
```

```tsx
function ComposerHarness({
  onSubmit,
  running = false,
  onStop,
}: {
  onSubmit(v: string): void;
  running?: boolean;
  onStop?(): void;
}) {
  const [value, setValue] = useState('');
```
