`packages/ui/src/agent/format.ts`:
```ts
import {
  CircleCheck,
  Compass,
  File,
  FileCode,
  FileDiff,
  FilePen,
  FileSpreadsheet,
  FileText,
  Flag,
  FolderTree,
  GitBranch,
  ListChecks,
  MessageCircleQuestion,
  MousePointerClick,
  Presentation,
  RotateCcw,
  Search,
  SquareTerminal,
  Wrench,
  type LucideIcon,
} from 'lucide-preact';

/** «12s», «1m 27s», «2m», «1h 5m» — как «Working for …» в ответе агента. */
export function formatDuration(totalSeconds: number): string {
  const seconds = Number.isFinite(totalSeconds) && totalSeconds > 0 ? Math.floor(totalSeconds) : 0;
  if (seconds < 60) return `${seconds}s`;
  if (seconds < 3600) {
    const rest = seconds % 60;
    return rest ? `${Math.floor(seconds / 60)}m ${rest}s` : `${Math.floor(seconds / 60)}m`;
  }
  const minutes = Math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60);
  return minutes ? `${Math.floor(seconds / 3600)}h ${minutes}m` : `${Math.floor(seconds / 3600)}h`;
}

export type FileKind = 'sheet' | 'doc' | 'slides' | 'code' | 'other';

const KIND_BY_EXTENSION: Record<string, FileKind> = {
  csv: 'sheet',
  tsv: 'sheet',
  xls: 'sheet',
  xlsx: 'sheet',
  ods: 'sheet',
  md: 'doc',
  txt: 'doc',
  pdf: 'doc',
  doc: 'doc',
  docx: 'doc',
  rtf: 'doc',
  odt: 'doc',
  ppt: 'slides',
  pptx: 'slides',
  key: 'slides',
  odp: 'slides',
  py: 'code',
  js: 'code',
  ts: 'code',
  tsx: 'code',
  jsx: 'code',
  json: 'code',
  sh: 'code',
  sql: 'code',
  html: 'code',
  css: 'code',
  yaml: 'code',
  yml: 'code',
  ipynb: 'code',
};

/** Вид файла по расширению — для цвета и иконки карточки файла. */
export function fileKind(name: string): FileKind {
  const dot = name.lastIndexOf('.');
  return (dot > 0 && KIND_BY_EXTENSION[name.slice(dot + 1).toLowerCase()]) || 'other';
}

export const FILE_KIND_ICONS: Record<FileKind, LucideIcon> = {
  sheet: FileSpreadsheet,
  doc: FileText,
  slides: Presentation,
  code: FileCode,
  other: File,
};

/** Фиксированные иконки инструментов агента (дизайн-система, «Иконки»). */
export const TOOL_ICONS: Record<string, LucideIcon> = {
  web_search: Search,
  browser_open: Compass,
  browser_scroll: Compass,
  browser_click: MousePointerClick,
  browser_type: MousePointerClick,
  shell: SquareTerminal,
  file_read: FileText,
  file_write: FilePen,
  file_edit: FileDiff,
  file_list: FolderTree,
  ask_user: MessageCircleQuestion,
  create_plan: ListChecks,
  report: CircleCheck,
  accept: CircleCheck,
  revise: RotateCcw,
  replan: GitBranch,
  finish: Flag,
};

export function toolIcon(tool: string): LucideIcon {
  return TOOL_ICONS[tool] ?? Wrench;
}
```
`packages/ui/src/agent/composer.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ComponentChildren } from 'preact';
import { useRef } from 'preact/hooks';
import { ArrowUp, Square, X, type LucideIcon } from 'lucide-preact';
import { useAutosize } from '../lib/autosize.ts';
import { FILE_KIND_ICONS, fileKind } from './format.ts';

export interface ComposerProps {
  value: string;
  onValueChange(value: string): void;
  onSubmit(value: string): void;
  /** Агент работает: вместо отправки — кнопка «стоп». */
  running?: boolean;
  onStop?(): void;
  label?: string;
  placeholder?: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
  /** Чипы над полем (облако, проект). */
  context?: ComponentChildren;
  /** Вложения (`<Attachment>`). */
  attachments?: ComponentChildren;
  /** Кнопки слева в нижней полосе (прикрепить, инструменты). */
  tools?: ComponentChildren;
  /** Кнопки справа перед отправкой (голос). */
  actions?: ComponentChildren;
}

/** Поле задачи: растёт по содержимому, Enter отправляет, Shift+Enter — новая строка. */
export function Composer({
  value,
  onValueChange,
  onSubmit,
  running = false,
  onStop,
  label = 'Task',
  placeholder = 'Give your agent a task',
  disabled = false,
  context,
  attachments,
  tools,
  actions,
}: ComposerProps) {
  const input = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);
  useAutosize(input, value);
  const canSend = !disabled && !running && value.trim().length > 0;
  const submit = () => {
    if (canSend) onSubmit(value);
  };
  return (
    <div class="ui-composer">
      {context && <div class="ui-composer__ctx">{context}</div>}
      {attachments && <div class="ui-composer__attachments">{attachments}</div>}
      <textarea
        ref={input}
        class="ui-composer__input"
        rows={2}
        aria-label={label}
        placeholder={placeholder}
        value={value}
        disabled={disabled}
        onInput={event => onValueChange(event.currentTarget.value)}
        onKeyDown={event => {
          if (event.key !== 'Enter' || event.shiftKey || event.isComposing) return;
          event.preventDefault();
          submit();
        }}
      />
      <div class="ui-composer__bar">
        {tools}
        <span class="ui-spacer" />
        {actions}
        {running ? (
          <button type="button" class="ui-send" data-state="stop" aria-label="Stop" onClick={onStop}>
            <Square aria-hidden="true" />
          </button>
        ) : (
          <button
            type="button"
            class="ui-send"
            data-state={canSend ? 'ready' : undefined}
            aria-label="Send"
            disabled={!canSend}
            onClick={submit}
          >
            <ArrowUp aria-hidden="true" />
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export interface AttachmentProps {
  name: string;
  /** «Spreadsheet · 84 KB». */
  meta?: string;
  icon?: LucideIcon;
  onRemove?(): void;
}

export function Attachment({ name, meta, icon, onRemove }: AttachmentProps) {
  const Icon = icon ?? FILE_KIND_ICONS[fileKind(name)];
  return (
    <div class="ui-attachment">
      <span class="ui-attachment__thumb">
        <Icon aria-hidden="true" />
      </span>
      <div class="ui-truncate">
        <b class="ui-truncate" title={name}>
          {name}
        </b>
        {meta && <small>{meta}</small>}
      </div>
      {onRemove && (
        <button type="button" class="ui-attachment__remove" aria-label={`Remove ${name}`} onClick={onRemove}>
          <X aria-hidden="true" />
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { ComponentChildren } from 'preact';
import { useId, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { ArrowRight, ArrowUp, ChevronRight, CornerDownRight, MessageCircleQuestion } from 'lucide-preact';
import { Button, IconButton } from '../components/button.tsx';
import { StatusDot } from '../components/display.tsx';
import { Input } from '../components/input.tsx';
import { FILE_KIND_ICONS, fileKind, formatDuration, type FileKind } from './format.ts';

export function UserMessage({ children }: { children: ComponentChildren }) {
  return <div class="ui-msg-user">{children}</div>;
}

export interface AgentMessageProps {
  /** Секунды с начала работы (или общее время, когда закончил). */
  elapsed: number;
  working: boolean;
  name?: string;
  /** Знак перед именем (логотип). */
  mark?: ComponentChildren;
  /** Бейдж после имени, например модель. */
  badge?: ComponentChildren;
  /** «Worked for … ›» раскрывает подробности хода работы. */
  onShowDetails?(): void;
  children?: ComponentChildren;
}

/** Ответ агента: шапка, «Working for 12s» → «Worked for 1m 27s ›», содержимое. */
export function AgentMessage({
  elapsed,
  working,
  name = 'agent',
  mark,
  badge,
  onShowDetails,
  children,
}: AgentMessageProps) {
  const time = formatDuration(elapsed);
  return (
    <div class="ui-agent">
      <div class="ui-agent__head">
        {mark}
        <span class="ui-agent__name">{name}</span>
        {badge}
      </div>
      {working ? (
        <span class="ui-agent__meta">Working for {time}</span>
      ) : onShowDetails ? (
        <button type="button" class="ui-agent__meta" onClick={onShowDetails}>
          Worked for {time}
          <ChevronRight aria-hidden="true" class="ui-icon-sm" />
        </button>
      ) : (
        <span class="ui-agent__meta">Worked for {time}</span>
      )}
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

export function AgentText({ children }: { children: ComponentChildren }) {
  return <p class="ui-agent__text">{children}</p>;
}

/** Индикатор «агент думает»: пульсирующая точка и бегущий блик по тексту. */
export function Thinking({ label = 'Thinking…' }: { label?: string }) {
  return (
    <span class="ui-thinking" role="status">
      <StatusDot tone="info" pulse />
      <span class="ui-shimmer">{label}</span>
    </span>
  );
}

export interface AskCardProps {
  question: ComponentChildren;
  /** Готовые ответы кнопками. */
  options?: readonly string[];
  onAnswer(answer: string): void;
  heading?: string;
  placeholder?: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

/** Вопрос агента пользователю (ask_user): варианты ответа и поле для своего. */
export function AskCard({
  question,
  options = [],
  onAnswer,
  heading = 'Your agent needs an answer',
  placeholder = 'Or type your answer…',
  disabled = false,
}: AskCardProps) {
  const headingId = `${useId()}-ask`;
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState('');
  const send = () => {
    const answer = draft.trim();
    if (!answer || disabled) return;
    onAnswer(answer);
    setDraft('');
  };
  return (
    <section class="ui-ask" aria-labelledby={headingId}>
      <div class="ui-ask__head" id={headingId}>
        <MessageCircleQuestion aria-hidden="true" class="ui-icon-sm" />
        {heading}
      </div>
      <div class="ui-ask__q">{question}</div>
      {options.length > 0 && (
        <div class="ui-ask__options">
          {options.map(option => (
            <Button key={option} size="sm" disabled={disabled} onClick={() => onAnswer(option)}>
              {option}
            </Button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      <Input
        aria-label="Your answer"
        placeholder={placeholder}
        value={draft}
        disabled={disabled}
        onInput={event => setDraft(event.currentTarget.value)}
        onKeyDown={event => {
          if (event.key !== 'Enter' || event.isComposing) return;
          event.preventDefault();
          send();
        }}
        action={
          <IconButton
            icon={ArrowUp}
            label="Send answer"
            variant="primary"
            pill
            disabled={disabled || !draft.trim()}
            onClick={send}
          />
        }
      />
    </section>
  );
}

export interface FileCardProps {
  name: string;
  /** «Spreadsheet · 394 B». */
  meta?: string;
  /** По умолчанию — по расширению имени. */
  kind?: FileKind;
  /** Ссылка на скачивание; без неё карточка — кнопка с onOpen. */
  href?: string;
  onOpen?(): void;
}

export function FileCard({ name, meta, kind, href, onOpen }: FileCardProps) {
  const resolved = kind ?? fileKind(name);
  const Icon = FILE_KIND_ICONS[resolved];
  const content = (
    <>
      <span class="ui-file__icon" data-kind={resolved === 'other' ? undefined : resolved}>
        <Icon aria-hidden="true" />
      </span>
      <span class="ui-file__body">
        <span class="ui-file__name" title={name}>
          {name}
        </span>
        {meta && <span class="ui-file__meta">{meta}</span>}
      </span>
    </>
  );
  return href ? (
    <a class="ui-file" href={href}>
      {content}
    </a>
  ) : (
    <button type="button" class="ui-file" onClick={onOpen}>
      {content}
    </button>
  );
}

/** Предложенный следующий шаг под ответом. */
export function Suggestion({ children, onSelect }: { children: ComponentChildren; onSelect(): void }) {
  return (
    <button type="button" class="ui-suggestion" onClick={onSelect}>
      <CornerDownRight aria-hidden="true" class="ui-icon-sm" />
      {children}
      <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" class="ui-icon-sm" />
    </button>
  );
}
```

`packages/ui/src/agent/run.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ComponentChildren } from 'preact';
import { useId, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import {
  ChevronDown,
  CircleAlert,
  CircleCheck,
  MessageCircleQuestion,
  MessageSquare,
  Wrench,
  type LucideIcon,
} from 'lucide-preact';
import { Spinner } from '../components/display.tsx';
import { cx } from '../lib/cx.ts';
import { TOOL_ICONS } from './format.ts';

/** Статус строки плана. Перевод статусов подзадач ядра — в веб-приложении (план 6). */
export type PlanStatus = 'pending' | 'running' | 'done' | 'failed' | 'skipped';

export interface PlanItem {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  status: PlanStatus;
}

const PLAN_STATUS_TEXT: Record<PlanStatus, string> = {
  pending: 'Pending',
  running: 'In progress',
  done: 'Done',
  failed: 'Failed',
  skipped: 'Skipped',
};

/** План подзадач: маркер статуса слева, текущая подзадача — `aria-current="step"`. */
export function PlanList({ items, label = 'Plan' }: { items: readonly PlanItem[]; label?: string }) {
  return (
    <div class="ui-timeline" role="list" aria-label={label}>
      {items.map(item => (
        <div
          key={item.id}
          class="ui-timeline__item"
          role="listitem"
          data-status={item.status}
          aria-current={item.status === 'running' ? 'step' : undefined}
        >
          <span class="ui-timeline__marker" aria-hidden="true" />
          <div>
            <div class="ui-timeline__title">
              <span class="ui-sr-only">{PLAN_STATUS_TEXT[item.status]}: </span>
              {item.title}
            </div>
            {item.description && (
              <div class={cx('ui-timeline__desc', item.status === 'running' && 'ui-shimmer')}>{item.description}</div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export interface StepGroupProps {
  /** «Searched 3 queries · Browsed 5 pages». */
  summary: string;
  /** Управляемый режим; без него группа помнит состояние сама. */
  expanded?: boolean;
  defaultExpanded?: boolean;
  onExpandedChange?(expanded: boolean): void;
  children: ComponentChildren;
}

/** Группа шагов, свёрнутая в строку-сводку (дизайн-система, «Сводка → подробности»). */
export function StepGroup({ summary, expanded, defaultExpanded = true, onExpandedChange, children }: StepGroupProps) {
  const listId = `${useId()}-steps`;
  const [own, setOwn] = useState(defaultExpanded);
  const open = expanded ?? own;
  return (
    <div class="ui-steps">
      <button
        type="button"
        class="ui-steps__summary"
        aria-expanded={open}
        aria-controls={listId}
        onClick={() => {
          setOwn(!open);
          onExpandedChange?.(!open);
        }}
      >
        <span>{summary}</span>
        <ChevronDown aria-hidden="true" />
      </button>
      <div id={listId} class="ui-steps__list" role="list">
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export interface StepProps {
  /** Имя инструмента агента: от него зависит иконка. */
  tool?: string;
  icon?: LucideIcon;
  children: string;
  /** «0:42». */
  time?: string;
  /** Текущий шаг — с бегущим бликом. */
  active?: boolean;
  error?: boolean;
}

export function Step({ tool, icon, children, time, active = false, error = false }: StepProps) {
  // Поиск по таблице, а не вызов toolIcon(): компонент не должен «создаваться» во время рендера.
  const Icon = icon ?? (tool ? TOOL_ICONS[tool] : undefined) ?? Wrench;
  return (
    <div class={cx('ui-step', error && 'ui-step--error')} role="listitem">
      <Icon aria-hidden="true" />
      <span class={cx(active && 'ui-shimmer')} title={children}>
        {children}
      </span>
      {time && <span class="ui-step__time">{time}</span>}
    </div>
  );
}

export type TaskItemStatus = 'running' | 'waiting' | 'done' | 'failed' | 'idle';

const TASK_STATUS: Record<Exclude<TaskItemStatus, 'running'>, [LucideIcon, string]> = {
  waiting: [MessageCircleQuestion, 'needs your answer'],
  done: [CircleCheck, 'completed'],
  failed: [CircleAlert, 'failed'],
  idle: [MessageSquare, 'idle'],
};

export interface TaskItemProps {
  title: string;
  status: TaskItemStatus;
  href: string;
  /** Открытая сейчас задача. */
  current?: boolean;
  /** Меню задачи (появляется при наведении). */
  menu?: ComponentChildren;
}

/** Задача в сайдбаре: статус слева, название-ссылка, меню при наведении. */
export function TaskItem({ title, status, href, current = false, menu }: TaskItemProps) {
  const [Icon, text] = status === 'running' ? [null, 'running'] : TASK_STATUS[status];
  return (
    <div class="ui-task-item">
      <span class="ui-task-item__status" data-status={status} aria-hidden="true">
        {Icon ? <Icon class="ui-icon-lg" /> : <Spinner size="sm" tone="info" />}
      </span>
      <a class="ui-task-item__title" href={href} aria-current={current ? 'page' : undefined}>
        {title}
        <span class="ui-sr-only">, {text}</span>
      </a>
      {menu}
    </div>
  );
}
```
`packages/ui/src/agent/computer.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ComponentChildren } from 'preact';
import { Maximize2, Monitor, Play, SkipBack, SkipForward, Sparkles, X } from 'lucide-preact';
import { Button, IconButton } from '../components/button.tsx';
import { Progress } from '../components/display.tsx';

export interface ComputerPanelProps {
  title?: string;
  /** «Using Browser», «Using Terminal». */
  activity?: string;
  /** Адрес страницы или путь файла. */
  detail?: string;
  /** Снимок экрана: Markdown страницы или файл моноширинным. */
  children?: ComponentChildren;
  /** Положение в ленте шагов, 0–100. */
  position?: number;
  /** Показывается последний шаг. */
  live?: boolean;
  onPrevious?(): void;
  onNext?(): void;
  onJumpToLive?(): void;
  onExpand?(): void;
  onClose?(): void;
}

/** Панель «компьютера агента»: вкладка, строка состояния, снимок и таймлайн перемотки. */
export function ComputerPanel({
  title = "Agent's computer",
  activity,
  detail,
  children,
  position = 100,
  live = true,
  onPrevious,
  onNext,
  onJumpToLive,
  onExpand,
  onClose,
}: ComputerPanelProps) {
  const value = Math.min(Math.max(position, 0), 100);
  return (
    <section class="ui-computer" aria-label={title}>
      <div class="ui-computer__tabs">
        <div class="ui-computer__tab">
          <Monitor aria-hidden="true" class="ui-icon-sm" />
          {title}
        </div>
        <span class="ui-spacer" />
        {onExpand && <IconButton icon={Maximize2} label="Expand" onClick={onExpand} />}
        {onClose && <IconButton icon={X} label="Close" onClick={onClose} />}
      </div>
      {activity && (
        <div class="ui-computer__status">
          <b>{activity}</b>
          {detail}
        </div>
      )}
      {/* Прокручиваемая область должна быть доступна с клавиатуры. */}
      {/* oxlint-disable-next-line jsx-a11y/no-noninteractive-tabindex */}
      <div class="ui-computer__body" role="region" aria-label={`${title}: screen`} tabIndex={0}>
        {children}
      </div>
      <div class="ui-computer__foot">
        <IconButton icon={SkipBack} label="Previous step" size="xs" disabled={!onPrevious} onClick={onPrevious} />
        <IconButton icon={SkipForward} label="Next step" size="xs" disabled={!onNext} onClick={onNext} />
        <div
          class="ui-timeline-bar"
          role="progressbar"
          aria-label="Replay position"
          aria-valuemin={0}
          aria-valuemax={100}
          aria-valuenow={Math.round(value)}
        >
          <i style={{ '--val': `${value}%` }} />
        </div>
        {live ? (
          <span class="ui-live">Live</span>
        ) : (
          <Button variant="ghost" size="xs" icon={Play} onClick={onJumpToLive}>
            Jump to live
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

export interface CreditBalanceProps {
  value: number;
  /** Объём, от которого считается полоса. Без него полосы нет. */
  total?: number;
  label?: string;
  hint?: ComponentChildren;
  format?(value: number): string;
}

const formatCreditValue = (value: number) => value.toLocaleString('en-US', { maximumFractionDigits: 3 });

/** Баланс кредитов: «996 / 1,300», полоса (жёлтая, когда осталось ≤ 10 %) и подсказка. */
export function CreditBalance({
  value,
  total,
  label = 'Credits',
  hint,
  format = formatCreditValue,
}: CreditBalanceProps) {
  const low = total !== undefined && total > 0 && value / total <= 0.1;
  return (
    <div class="ui-meter">
      <div class="ui-meter__head">
        <span class="ui-label">
          <Sparkles aria-hidden="true" class="ui-icon-sm" />
          {label}
        </span>
        <span class="ui-meter__value">
          {format(value)}
          {total !== undefined && <small> / {format(total)}</small>}
        </span>
      </div>
      {total !== undefined && <Progress label={label} value={value} max={total} tone={low ? 'warning' : 'accent'} />}
      {hint && <span class="ui-hint">{hint}</span>}
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 10: Фон входа `DotField`

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/ui/src/effects/dot-field.ts` (перенос `design/dot-field.js`), `packages/ui/src/effects/DotField.tsx`
- Modify: `packages/ui/src/index.ts`
- Test: `packages/ui/test/dot-field.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `cx`, `prefersReducedMotion`, `useReducedMotion` (Tasks 1–2); класс `ui-dot-field`, токены `--bg-void`, `--field-dot`, `--field-glow`, `--field-1…5` (Tasks 2–3).
- Produces:
```ts
export interface DotFieldOptions { pointerTarget?: HTMLElement | null; reducedMotion?: boolean }
export interface DotFieldHandle { refresh(): void; destroy(): void }
export function mountDotField(canvas: HTMLCanvasElement, options?: DotFieldOptions): DotFieldHandle | null; // null — нет 2D-контекста
export function DotField(props: { class?: string }): JSX.Element; // <canvas class="ui-dot-field" aria-hidden="true">
```

Алгоритм — как в `design/dot-field.js` (сетка 14 px, точка 1 px, пять полей по фигурам Лиссажу, подсветка 180 px с инерцией, маска `destination-in`, виньетка, DPR ≤ 2, пауза вне экрана и на скрытой вкладке, один кадр при reduced motion). Отличия: TypeScript; `null` без 2D-контекста; фон сам перечитывает токены при смене `data-theme` на `<html>` и системной темы (`MutationObserver` + `change` у `prefers-color-scheme`), ручной `refresh()` остаётся; `reducedMotion` можно передать явно.

- [ ] **Step 1: Падающие тесты**

`packages/ui/test/dot-field.test.tsx`:
```tsx
import { afterEach, expect, spyOn, test } from 'bun:test';
import { render } from '@testing-library/preact';
import { DotField, mountDotField } from '../src/index.ts';

// happy-dom не рисует на canvas (getContext → null), поэтому контекст подменяется записывающим двойником.
function fakeContext() {
  const calls = { drawImage: 0, fillRect: 0 };
  const context = {
    calls,
    globalCompositeOperation: 'source-over',
    globalAlpha: 1,
    fillStyle: '',
    createPattern: () => ({}),
    createRadialGradient: () => ({ addColorStop() {} }),
    clearRect() {},
    beginPath() {},
    arc() {},
    fill() {},
    fillRect() {
      calls.fillRect++;
    },
    drawImage() {
      calls.drawImage++;
    },
  };
  return context;
}

const restore: Array<() => void> = [];
afterEach(() => {
  while (restore.length) restore.pop()!();
});

function withCanvas() {
  const contexts: Array<ReturnType<typeof fakeContext>> = [];
  const spy = spyOn(HTMLCanvasElement.prototype, 'getContext').mockImplementation(((): unknown => {
    const context = fakeContext();
    contexts.push(context);
    return context;
  }) as never);
  restore.push(() => spy.mockRestore());
  const host = document.createElement('div');
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  host.append(canvas);
  document.body.append(host);
  restore.push(() => host.remove());
  // Первый контекст — у видимого canvas: только он рисует слой полей (drawImage).
  return { canvas, frames: () => contexts[0]!.calls.drawImage };
}

function spyFrames() {
  const raf = spyOn(globalThis, 'requestAnimationFrame').mockImplementation(() => 1);
  const cancel = spyOn(globalThis, 'cancelAnimationFrame').mockImplementation(() => {});
  restore.push(
    () => raf.mockRestore(),
    () => cancel.mockRestore(),
  );
  return { raf, cancel };
}

test('without a 2D context mountDotField returns null and DotField renders a decorative canvas', () => {
  expect(mountDotField(document.createElement('canvas'))).toBeNull();
  const { container, unmount } = render(<DotField />);
  const canvas = container.querySelector('canvas')!;
  expect(canvas.getAttribute('aria-hidden')).toBe('true');
  expect(canvas.className).toBe('ui-dot-field');
  unmount();
});

test('with reduced motion the field draws one still frame, never animates and redraws on a theme change', async () => {
  const { canvas, frames } = withCanvas();
  const { raf } = spyFrames();
  const field = mountDotField(canvas, { reducedMotion: true })!;
  expect(frames()).toBe(1);
  expect(raf).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  document.documentElement.dataset.theme = 'light';
  await Promise.resolve();
  expect(frames()).toBe(2);
  field.destroy();
  document.documentElement.dataset.theme = 'dark';
  await Promise.resolve();
  expect(frames()).toBe(2);
});

test('the animation pauses on a hidden tab, resumes when visible and stops for good on destroy', () => {
  const { canvas } = withCanvas();
  const { raf, cancel } = spyFrames();
  let hidden = false;
  Object.defineProperty(document, 'hidden', { configurable: true, get: () => hidden });
  restore.push(() => delete (document as { hidden?: boolean }).hidden);

  const field = mountDotField(canvas, { reducedMotion: false })!;
  expect(raf).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  hidden = true;
  document.dispatchEvent(new Event('visibilitychange'));
  expect(cancel).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  hidden = false;
  document.dispatchEvent(new Event('visibilitychange'));
  expect(raf).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  field.destroy();
  expect(cancel).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  document.dispatchEvent(new Event('visibilitychange'));
  expect(raf).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Движок и компонент**

`packages/ui/src/effects/dot-field.ts`:
```ts
/**
 * «Поле точек» — анимированный фон экрана входа (перенос design/dot-field.js).
 * Сетка точек, сквозь которую видны медленно плывущие цветовые поля, и мягкая подсветка
 * у курсора. Цвета — из токенов --bg-void, --field-dot, --field-glow, --field-1…5; при смене
 * темы (data-theme на <html> или системной темы) фон перечитывает их сам.
 */
import { prefersReducedMotion } from '../lib/media.ts';

const STEP = 14; // шаг сетки, px
const DOT = 1; // радиус точки, px
const GLOW_RADIUS = 180; // радиус подсветки у курсора, px
const STILL_FRAME = 12000; // момент времени для единственного кадра при reduced motion, мс

interface ColorField {
  color: string;
  ax: number;
  ay: number;
  fx: number;
  fy: number;
  px: number;
  py: number;
  r: number;
  fr: number;
}

export interface DotFieldOptions {
  /** Где слушать указатель; по умолчанию — родитель canvas. */
  pointerTarget?: HTMLElement | null;
  /** По умолчанию — prefers-reduced-motion. */
  reducedMotion?: boolean;
}

export interface DotFieldHandle {
  /** Перечитать цвета из токенов (смену data-theme фон замечает сам). */
  refresh(): void;
  /** Остановить анимацию и снять все обработчики. */
  destroy(): void;
}

/** Запускает фон на canvas. `null` — у браузера нет 2D-контекста (фон просто не рисуется). */
export function mountDotField(canvas: HTMLCanvasElement, options: DotFieldOptions = {}): DotFieldHandle | null {
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  const layer = document.createElement('canvas');
  const lctx = layer.getContext('2d');
  if (!ctx || !lctx) return null;
  const reduced = options.reducedMotion ?? prefersReducedMotion();
  const pointerTarget = options.pointerTarget ?? canvas.parentElement ?? canvas;

  let dpr = 1;
  let maskPattern: CanvasPattern | string = 'transparent';
  let dimPattern: CanvasPattern | string = 'transparent';
  let voidColor = '#000000';
  let glow = '255,255,255';
  let fields: ColorField[] = [];
  const pointer = { x: 0, y: 0, tx: 0, ty: 0, a: 0, ta: 0, seen: false };

  function dotTile(color: string): HTMLCanvasElement {
    const tile = document.createElement('canvas');
    tile.width = tile.height = Math.round(STEP * dpr);
    const t = tile.getContext('2d');
    if (t) {
      t.fillStyle = color;
      t.beginPath();
      t.arc(tile.width / 2, tile.height / 2, DOT * dpr, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      t.fill();
    }
    return tile;
  }

  function readTheme() {
    const css = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement);
    voidColor = css.getPropertyValue('--bg-void').trim() || voidColor;
    const dotColor = css.getPropertyValue('--field-dot').trim();
    glow = css.getPropertyValue('--field-glow').trim().split(/\s+/).join(',') || glow;
    // Каждое поле дрейфует по своей фигуре Лиссажу (периоды 40–70 с) и медленно «дышит».
    fields = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((n, i) => ({
      color: css.getPropertyValue(`--field-${n}`).trim(),
      ax: 0.32 + 0.05 * i,
      ay: 0.26 + 0.04 * ((i * 3) % 5),
      fx: (2 * Math.PI) / (40 + i * 7),
      fy: (2 * Math.PI) / (52 + i * 5),
      px: i * 1.7,
      py: i * 2.3,
      r: 0.36 + 0.05 * (i % 3),
      fr: (2 * Math.PI) / (18 + i * 4),
    }));
    maskPattern = lctx!.createPattern(dotTile('#fff'), 'repeat') ?? 'transparent';
    dimPattern = ctx!.createPattern(dotTile(dotColor), 'repeat') ?? 'transparent';
  }

  function resize() {
    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    dpr = Math.min(devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
    for (const c of [canvas, layer]) {
      c.width = Math.round(rect.width * dpr);
      c.height = Math.round(rect.height * dpr);
    }
    readTheme();
  }

  function frame(ms: number) {
    const t = ms / 1000;
    const W = canvas.width;
    const H = canvas.height;
    const m = Math.min(W, H);
    const l = lctx!;
    const c = ctx!;

    // 1. Цветовые поля
    l.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
    l.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
    l.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
    for (const f of fields) {
      const x = W * (0.5 + f.ax * Math.sin(t * f.fx + f.px));
      const y = H * (0.5 + f.ay * Math.cos(t * f.fy + f.py));
      const r = m * f.r * (1 + 0.12 * Math.sin(t * f.fr));
      const g = l.createRadialGradient(x, y, 0, x, y, r);
      g.addColorStop(0, `${f.color}cc`);
      g.addColorStop(0.5, `${f.color}55`);
      g.addColorStop(1, `${f.color}00`);
      l.fillStyle = g;
      l.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
    }

    // 2. Подсветка у курсора с инерцией
    pointer.x += (pointer.tx - pointer.x) * 0.08;
    pointer.y += (pointer.ty - pointer.y) * 0.08;
    pointer.a += (pointer.ta - pointer.a) * 0.06;
    if (pointer.a > 0.01) {
      const px = pointer.x * dpr;
      const py = pointer.y * dpr;
      const g = l.createRadialGradient(px, py, 0, px, py, GLOW_RADIUS * dpr);
      g.addColorStop(0, `rgba(${glow},${0.75 * pointer.a})`);
      g.addColorStop(1, `rgba(${glow},0)`);
      l.fillStyle = g;
      l.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
    }

    // 3. Цвет остаётся только там, где точки
    l.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-in';
    l.fillStyle = maskPattern;
    l.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

    // 4. Сборка: фон → тусклые точки → цветные точки → виньетка
    c.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
    c.fillStyle = voidColor;
    c.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
    c.fillStyle = dimPattern;
    c.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
    c.globalAlpha = 0.9;
    c.drawImage(layer, 0, 0);
    c.globalAlpha = 1;
    const v = c.createRadialGradient(W / 2, H / 2, m * 0.18, W / 2, H / 2, Math.hypot(W, H) / 2);
    v.addColorStop(0, `${voidColor}b3`);
    v.addColorStop(0.35, `${voidColor}00`);
    v.addColorStop(1, `${voidColor}e6`);
    c.fillStyle = v;
    c.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  }

  let raf: number | null = null;
  let visible = true;
  const loop = (ms: number) => {
    frame(ms);
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  };
  const start = () => {
    if (raf === null && visible && !document.hidden && !reduced) raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  };
  const stop = () => {
    if (raf !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
    raf = null;
  };

  const onMove = (event: PointerEvent) => {
    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    pointer.tx = event.clientX - rect.left;
    pointer.ty = event.clientY - rect.top;
    pointer.ta = 1;
    if (!pointer.seen) {
      pointer.x = pointer.tx;
      pointer.y = pointer.ty;
      pointer.seen = true;
    }
  };
  const onLeave = () => {
    pointer.ta = 0;
  };
  const onResize = () => {
    resize();
    if (reduced) frame(STILL_FRAME);
  };
  const onVisibility = () => (document.hidden ? stop() : start());
  const onTheme = () => {
    readTheme();
    if (reduced) frame(STILL_FRAME);
  };
  const themeObserver = new MutationObserver(onTheme);
  const systemTheme = matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: light)');
  const io = new IntersectionObserver(([entry]) => {
    visible = entry?.isIntersecting ?? true;
    if (visible) start();
    else stop();
  });

  resize();
  addEventListener('resize', onResize);
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
  themeObserver.observe(document.documentElement, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['data-theme'] });
  systemTheme.addEventListener('change', onTheme);
  io.observe(canvas);
  if (reduced) frame(STILL_FRAME);
  else {
    pointerTarget.addEventListener('pointermove', onMove);
    pointerTarget.addEventListener('pointerleave', onLeave);
    start();
  }

  return {
    refresh: onTheme,
    destroy() {
      stop();
      io.disconnect();
      themeObserver.disconnect();
      systemTheme.removeEventListener('change', onTheme);
      removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
      pointerTarget.removeEventListener('pointermove', onMove);
      pointerTarget.removeEventListener('pointerleave', onLeave);
    },
  };
}
```
`packages/ui/src/effects/DotField.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'preact/hooks';
import { cx } from '../lib/cx.ts';
import { useReducedMotion } from '../lib/media.ts';
import { mountDotField } from './dot-field.ts';

export interface DotFieldProps {
  class?: string;
}

/**
 * Анимированный фон входа. Canvas занимает родителя (родителю — `position: relative`), следует
 * теме, при reduced motion рисует один кадр, вне экрана и на скрытой вкладке стоит,
 * при размонтировании снимает обработчики.
 */
export function DotField({ class: className }: DotFieldProps) {
  const canvas = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
  const reduced = useReducedMotion();
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!canvas.current) return;
    const field = mountDotField(canvas.current, { reducedMotion: reduced });
    return () => field?.destroy();
  }, [reduced]);
  return <canvas ref={canvas} class={cx('ui-dot-field', className)} aria-hidden="true" />;
}
```

- Produces:

```ts
// packages/ui/showcase/src/App.tsx
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Падающие тесты**

`packages/ui/test/showcase.test.tsx`:
```tsx
import { expect, test } from 'bun:test';
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/preact';
import * as ui from '../src/index.ts';
import { App, NAV } from '../showcase/src/App.tsx';

const root = new URL('../', import.meta.url).pathname;

test('the showcase renders the sign-in screen and every section from its navigation', () => {
  render(<App />);
  expect(screen.getByRole('region', { name: 'Sign in' })).toBeTruthy();
  for (const [, items] of NAV) {
    for (const [id, title] of items) {
      expect(document.getElementById(id)?.querySelector('h2')?.textContent).toBe(title);
    }
  }
});

test('every exported component appears in the showcase', async () => {
  const sources = await Promise.all(
    [...new Bun.Glob('showcase/src/**/*.tsx').scanSync(root)].map(file => Bun.file(root + file).text()),
  );
  const code = sources.join('\n');
  // Portal и PortalContext — инфраструктура поповеров, а не самостоятельные компоненты.
  const components = Object.entries(ui)
    .filter(([name, value]) => typeof value === 'function' && /^[A-Z]/.test(name) && !name.startsWith('Portal'))
    .map(([name]) => name);
  expect(components.filter(name => !code.includes(`<${name}`))).toEqual([]);
});
```
В `packages/ui/test/styles.test.ts` последний тест проверяет и витрину — заменить его целиком:
```ts
test('every ui-* class used by the components and the showcase exists in the stylesheets', async () => {
  const sources = ['src/**/*.tsx', 'showcase/src/**/*.tsx'].flatMap(glob => [...new Bun.Glob(glob).scanSync(root)]);
  const css = new Set([...styles.matchAll(/\.(ui-[\w-]+)/g)].map(m => m[1]!));
  const missing = new Set<string>();
  for (const file of sources) {
    // Комментарии не в счёт: «ui-kit» в JSDoc — не класс.
    const text = (await Bun.file(root + file).text()).replace(/\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g, '').replace(/^\s*\/\/.*$/gm, '');
    for (const m of text.matchAll(/['"`\s](ui-[a-z0-9_-]*[a-z0-9])(?=['"`\s])/g))
      if (!css.has(m[1]!)) missing.add(`${file}: ${m[1]}`);
  }
  expect([...missing]).toEqual([]);
});
```

```ts
import { preact } from '@preact/preset-vite';
import { defineConfig } from 'vite';
import { themeBootScript } from '../src/theme.ts';

// Витрина ui-kit: `bun run ui:dev` (http://localhost:5174), сборка — `bun run ui:build` в showcase/dist.
export default defineConfig({
  root: import.meta.dirname,
  plugins: [
    preact(),
    {
      name: 'theme-boot',
      // Тема применяется до первой отрисовки, как будет в веб-приложении.
      transformIndexHtml: () => [{ tag: 'script', children: themeBootScript, injectTo: 'head-prepend' }],
    },
  ],
  server: { port: 5174 },
  build: { outDir: 'dist', emptyOutDir: true },
});
```

`packages/ui/showcase/src/showcase.css`:
```css
/* Раскладка витрины (не часть ui-kit): перенесено из <style> design/preview.html */
html {
  background: var(--bg-app);
  scroll-behavior: smooth;
  scroll-padding-top: 72px;
}
.mark {
  width: 22px;
  height: 22px;
  border-radius: 7px;
  flex: none;
  background: conic-gradient(
    from 210deg,
    var(--field-1),
    var(--field-2),
    var(--field-5),
    var(--field-4),
    var(--field-3),
    var(--field-1)
  );
  position: relative;
}
.mark::after {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  inset: 6px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--bg-app);
}
.mark--sm {
  width: 18px;
  height: 18px;
  border-radius: 6px;
}
.mark--sm::after {
  inset: 5px;
}

.auth {
  position: relative;
  min-height: 100svh;
  background: var(--bg-void);
  overflow: hidden;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  padding: var(--space-6) var(--space-4);
}
.auth .mark::after {
  background: var(--bg-void);
}
.auth__brand {
  position: absolute;
  top: var(--space-5);
  left: var(--space-6);
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: var(--space-2);
  font: italic 24px/1 var(--font-display);
  z-index: 1;
}
.auth__theme {
  position: absolute;
  top: var(--space-4);
  right: var(--space-4);
  z-index: 1;
}
.auth__card {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1;
  width: 100%;
  max-width: var(--auth-width);
  text-align: center;
  animation: ui-rise var(--dur-slower) var(--ease-out) both 120ms;
  display: grid;
  gap: var(--space-2);
}
.auth__card h1 {
  margin: 0;
}
.auth__card h1 em {
  color: var(--accent-text);
}
.auth__lead {
  margin: 0 0 var(--space-6);
  color: var(--text-secondary);
  font-size: var(--text-md);
}
.auth__legal {
  margin: var(--space-4) 0 0;
  color: var(--text-tertiary);
  font-size: var(--text-xs);
  line-height: var(--leading-xs);
}

.doc-top {
  position: sticky;
  top: 0;
  z-index: var(--z-header);
  height: 56px;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: var(--space-3);
  padding: 0 var(--space-4);
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--bg-app) 85%, transparent);
  backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-subtle);
}
.doc-top .word {
  font: italic 20px/1 var(--font-display);
}
.doc-top .ui-spacer {
  flex: 1;
}
.doc {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 220px minmax(0, 1fr);
  max-width: 1280px;
  margin: 0 auto;
}
.doc-nav {
  position: sticky;
  top: 56px;
  align-self: start;
  height: calc(100svh - 56px);
  overflow: auto;
  padding: var(--space-6) var(--space-3);
  border-right: 1px solid var(--border-subtle);
}
.doc-main {
  padding: var(--space-10) var(--space-8) var(--space-20);
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
  gap: var(--space-16);
  min-width: 0;
  overflow-x: clip;
}
.doc-section > h2 {
  font: var(--weight-regular) var(--text-2xl)/var(--leading-2xl) var(--font-display);
  margin: 0 0 var(--space-1);
}
.doc-section > p {
  color: var(--text-secondary);
  margin: 0 0 var(--space-6);
  max-width: 680px;
}
.doc-sub {
  font-size: var(--text-sm);
  line-height: var(--leading-sm);
  font-weight: var(--weight-medium);
  color: var(--text-tertiary);
  margin: var(--space-8) 0 var(--space-3);
}
.doc-section > .doc-sub:first-of-type {
  margin-top: 0;
}
.doc-row {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: var(--space-3);
  align-items: center;
}
.doc-grid {
  display: grid;
  gap: var(--space-4);
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(260px, 1fr));
}
.doc-grid--2 {
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(300px, 1fr));
}
.doc-col {
  display: grid;
  gap: var(--space-4);
  align-content: start;
}
.doc-stage {
  padding: var(--space-6);
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
  border: 1px solid var(--border-subtle);
  background: var(--fill-subtle);
}

.swatches {
  display: grid;
  gap: var(--space-3);
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(140px, 1fr));
}
.swatch {
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  overflow: hidden;
  border: 1px solid var(--border-default);
  background: var(--bg-surface);
}
.swatch div {
  height: 52px;
}
.swatch span {
  display: block;
  padding: var(--space-2) var(--space-3);
  font: var(--text-xs)/var(--leading-xs) var(--font-mono);
  color: var(--text-secondary);
  word-break: break-all;
}
.type-row {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 140px 1fr;
  gap: var(--space-4);
  align-items: baseline;
  padding: var(--space-3) 0;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-subtle);
}
.type-row code {
  font: var(--text-xs)/var(--leading-xs) var(--font-mono);
  color: var(--text-tertiary);
}
.radius-demo {
  width: 64px;
  height: 64px;
  background: var(--bg-surface-raised);
  border: 1px solid var(--border-strong);
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  font: var(--text-xs) var(--font-mono);
  color: var(--text-tertiary);
}
.elev {
  height: 88px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
  background: var(--bg-surface);
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  font: var(--text-xs) var(--font-mono);
  color: var(--text-tertiary);
}
.motion-track {
  height: 4px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-full);
  background: var(--fill-active);
  position: relative;
  margin: 8px 0;
}
.motion-track i {
  position: absolute;
  left: 0;
  top: -6px;
  width: 16px;
  height: 16px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--accent);
}
.motion-run .motion-track i {
  left: calc(100% - 16px);
}
.icon-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(112px, 1fr));
  gap: var(--space-2);
}
.icon-cell {
  display: grid;
  justify-items: center;
  gap: var(--space-2);
  padding: var(--space-3) var(--space-1);
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  color: var(--icon-secondary);
  font: 11px/14px var(--font-mono);
  text-align: center;
}
.icon-cell span {
  color: var(--text-tertiary);
  word-break: break-all;
}

.shell {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: var(--sidebar-width) minmax(0, 1fr) minmax(0, 1fr);
  height: 680px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-xl);
  overflow: hidden;
  border: 1px solid var(--border-default);
  background: var(--bg-app);
}
.shell-side {
  background: var(--bg-sidebar);
  border-right: 1px solid var(--border-subtle);
  padding: var(--space-3) var(--space-2);
  overflow: hidden;
}
.shell-chat {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  min-width: 0;
  border-right: 1px solid var(--border-subtle);
}
.shell-body {
  flex: 1;
  overflow: auto;
  padding: var(--space-5);
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: var(--space-5);
}
.shell-foot {
  padding: 0 var(--space-3) var(--space-3);
}
.shell-foot small {
  display: block;
  text-align: center;
  color: var(--text-tertiary);
  font-size: var(--text-xs);
  margin-top: var(--space-2);
}
.settings-demo {
  max-width: 680px;
  display: grid;
  gap: var(--space-8);
}

@media (max-width: 1100px) {
  .shell {
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) minmax(0, 1fr);
  }
  .shell-side {
    display: none;
  }
}
@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .doc {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
  .doc-nav {
    display: none;
  }
  .doc-main {
    padding: var(--space-8) var(--space-4) var(--space-16);
  }
  .shell {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    height: auto;
  }
  .shell .ui-computer {
    min-height: 360px;
  }
}
```

`packages/ui/package.json` — после `"type": "module"`:

Корневой `package.json` — после `"check"`:
```json
    "ui:dev": "bun run --cwd packages/ui dev",
    "ui:build": "bun run --cwd packages/ui build",
```
`tsconfig.json`: `"include": ["packages/*/src", "packages/*/test", "packages/*/*.ts", "packages/ui/showcase", "scripts"]`.
`.gitignore` — строка `packages/ui/showcase/dist/`; `.prettierignore` — строка `packages/ui/showcase/dist`; `.oxlintrc.json` — в `ignorePatterns` добавить `"packages/ui/showcase/dist/**"`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Разделы витрины**

Разделы повторяют `design/preview.html`: вход, основы (цвета, типографика, отступы и тени, движение, иконки), кнопки, поля, выбор, навигация, оверлеи, обратная связь, данные, паттерны агента, экран задачи. Цвета основ читаются из токенов текущей темы. Экран задачи проигрывает прогон по шагам (план, шаги, «Thinking», компьютер, «стоп»).

`packages/ui/showcase/src/sections/auth.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { Monitor, Moon, Sun } from 'lucide-preact';
import { Badge, Button, DotField, Input, SegmentedControl, useTheme, useToast } from '../../../src/index.ts';

const EMAIL = /^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/;

/** Экран входа: фон «поле точек», переключатель темы, вход по OAuth и magic link. */
export function AuthSection() {
  const { theme, setTheme } = useTheme();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [sending, setSending] = useState(false);
  return (
    <section class="auth" aria-label="Sign in">
      <DotField />
      <div class="auth__brand">
        <span class="mark" />
        agent
      </div>
      <div class="auth__theme">
        <SegmentedControl
          label="Theme"
          value={theme}
          onChange={setTheme}
          options={[
            { value: 'light', icon: Sun, ariaLabel: 'Light theme' },
            { value: 'dark', icon: Moon, ariaLabel: 'Dark theme' },
            { value: 'auto', icon: Monitor, ariaLabel: 'System theme' },
          ]}
        />
      </div>
      <div class="auth__card">
        <h1 class="ui-display">
          Hand it <em>off.</em>
        </h1>
        <p class="auth__lead">Sign in to give your agent its first task.</p>
        <Button block>
          Continue with Google <Badge tone="accent">Last used</Badge>
        </Button>
        <Button block>Continue with GitHub</Button>
        <div class="ui-divider ui-divider--label">or</div>
        <Input
          type="email"
          aria-label="Email address"
          placeholder="you@company.com"
          value={email}
          onInput={e => setEmail(e.currentTarget.value)}
        />
        <Button
          variant="primary"
          block
          disabled={!EMAIL.test(email)}
          loading={sending}
          onClick={() => {
            setSending(true);
            setTimeout(() => {
              setSending(false);
              toast.show({
                tone: 'success',
                title: 'Check your inbox',
                description: `We sent a sign-in link to ${email}.`,
              });
            }, 1200);
          }}
        >
          Email me a sign-in link
        </Button>
        <p class="auth__legal">
          By continuing you agree to the{' '}
          <a class="ui-link--muted" href="#terms">
            Terms
          </a>
          .
        </p>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```
`packages/ui/showcase/src/sections/foundations.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { Play, Sparkles } from 'lucide-preact';
import { Button, cx, Spinner, StatusDot, TOOL_ICONS, useTheme } from '../../../src/index.ts';

const SWATCHES: Array<[string, string[]]> = [
  [
    'Surfaces',
    ['--bg-void', '--bg-panel', '--bg-sidebar', '--bg-app', '--bg-surface', '--bg-surface-raised', '--bg-inset'],
  ],
  [
    'Fills & borders',
    ['--fill-subtle', '--fill-muted', '--fill-hover', '--fill-active', '--border-default', '--border-strong'],
  ],
  ['Text', ['--text-primary', '--text-secondary', '--text-tertiary', '--text-quaternary', '--text-disabled']],
  ['Accent', ['--accent', '--accent-hover', '--accent-pressed', '--accent-soft', '--accent-text', '--accent-disabled']],
  [
    'Status',
    [
      '--success',
      '--success-soft',
      '--warning',
      '--warning-soft',
      '--danger',
      '--danger-soft',
      '--info',
      '--info-soft',
    ],
  ],
  ['Sign-in field', ['--field-1', '--field-2', '--field-3', '--field-4', '--field-5', '--field-dot']],
];

/** Значения токенов в текущей теме: перечитываются при её смене. */
function useTokenValues(names: string[]): Record<string, string> {
  const { resolved } = useTheme();
  const [values, setValues] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  useEffect(() => {
    const css = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement);
    setValues(Object.fromEntries(names.map(name => [name, css.getPropertyValue(name).trim()])));
  }, [resolved, names]);
  return values;
}

const ALL_SWATCHES = SWATCHES.flatMap(([, names]) => names);

export function ColorSection() {
  const values = useTokenValues(ALL_SWATCHES);
  return (
    <section class="doc-section" id="color">
      <h2>Color</h2>
      <p>Quiet monochrome chrome, one iris accent for actions, status colors only where they carry meaning.</p>
      {SWATCHES.map(([title, names]) => (
        <div key={title}>
          <div class="doc-sub">{title}</div>
          <div class="swatches">
            {names.map(name => (
              <div key={name} class="swatch">
                <div style={{ background: `var(${name})` }} />
                <span>
                  {name.slice(2)}
                  <br />
                  {values[name]}
                </span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
    </section>
  );
}

const TYPE_SCALE: Array<[string, string, string]> = [
  ['display 44/48 serif', 'ui-display', 'Your agent, on it.'],
  ['xl 22/28 · 500', 'ui-h1', 'Automations'],
  ['lg 18/26 · 500', 'ui-h2', 'Files in this task'],
  ['md 16/24 · 500', 'ui-h3', 'Appearance'],
  ['sm 13/18', 'ui-text-sm ui-text-secondary', 'Used to personalize responses across tasks.'],
  ['xs 12/16', 'ui-text-xs ui-text-tertiary', 'Agents can make mistakes. Check important results.'],
  ['mono 13/20', 'ui-mono ui-text-secondary', '$ python analyze.py --input data.csv'],
];

export function FoundationsSection() {
  const [run, setRun] = useState(false);
  return (
    <>
      <section class="doc-section" id="type">
        <h2>Typography</h2>
        <p>Geist for the interface, Instrument Serif for one expressive heading per screen, Geist Mono for code.</p>
        {TYPE_SCALE.map(([label, cls, sample]) => (
          <div key={label} class="type-row">
            <code>{label}</code>
            <div class={cls}>{sample}</div>
          </div>
        ))}
        <div class="type-row">
          <code>serif 28/34</code>
          <div class="ui-serif ui-truncate">Upgrade your plan</div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section class="doc-section" id="layout">
        <h2>Space, radius, depth</h2>
        <p>4px grid. Radii grow with the size of the surface. Elevation is a light edge ring plus a soft shadow.</p>
        <div class="doc-row">
          {[4, 6, 8, 10, 14, 20, 24, 9999].map(r => (
            <div key={r} class="radius-demo" style={{ borderRadius: `${r}px` }}>
              {r === 9999 ? 'full' : r}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div class="doc-grid" style={{ marginTop: 'var(--space-4)' }}>
          {['xs', 'sm', 'md', 'lg'].map(size => (
            <div key={size} class="elev" style={{ boxShadow: `var(--shadow-${size})` }}>
              {size}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section class="doc-section" id="motion">
        <h2>Motion</h2>
        <p>Appear with ease-out, disappear faster with ease-in. Only live indicators loop.</p>
        <Button size="sm" icon={Play} onClick={() => setRun(r => !r)}>
          Play
        </Button>
        <div class={cx('doc-grid', run && 'motion-run')} style={{ marginTop: 'var(--space-4)' }}>
          {(
            [
              ['Hover · 150ms', 'var(--dur-fast) var(--ease-standard)'],
              ['Menu · 200ms', 'var(--dur-base) var(--ease-out)'],
              ['Dialog · 320ms', 'var(--dur-slow) var(--ease-out)'],
              ['Toggle · spring', 'var(--dur-base) var(--ease-spring)'],
            ] as const
          ).map(([label, transition]) => (
            <div key={label} class="ui-card">
              <b>{label}</b>
              <div class="motion-track">
                <i style={{ transition: `left ${transition}` }} />
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
          <div class="ui-card doc-col">
            <b>Live indicators</b>
            <div class="doc-row">
              <StatusDot tone="info" pulse />
              <Spinner tone="info" />
              <span class="ui-shimmer">Reading page…</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section class="doc-section" id="icons">
        <h2>Icons</h2>
        <p>Lucide, 2px stroke (1.75 at 18px and up), sizes 12 · 14 · 16 · 18 · 20. Agent tools have fixed icons.</p>
        <div class="doc-row ui-text-secondary">
          {['ui-icon-xs', 'ui-icon-sm', '', 'ui-icon-lg', 'ui-icon-xl'].map(size => (
            <Sparkles key={size || 'md'} class={size} aria-hidden="true" />
          ))}
        </div>
        <div class="icon-grid" style={{ marginTop: 'var(--space-4)' }}>
          {Object.entries(TOOL_ICONS).map(([tool, Icon]) => (
            <div key={tool} class="icon-cell">
              <Icon class="ui-icon-xl" aria-hidden="true" />
              <span>{tool}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>
    </>
  );
}
```

```tsx
export function ButtonsSection() {
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  return (
    <section class="doc-section" id="buttons">
      <h2>Buttons</h2>
      <p>40px default, 10px radius. One primary per view. Loading uses aria-busy, disabled the native attribute.</p>
      <div class="doc-sub">Variants</div>
      <div class="doc-row">
        <Button variant="primary" icon={Sparkles}>
          Primary
        </Button>
        <Button>Secondary</Button>
        <Button variant="outline">Outline</Button>
        <Button variant="ghost">Ghost</Button>
        <Button variant="inverted" pill>
          Inverted
        </Button>
        <Button variant="danger">Danger</Button>
        <Button variant="danger-outline">Delete account</Button>
        <Button variant="danger-ghost">Remove</Button>
        <Button variant="link">Link button</Button>
      </div>
      <div class="doc-sub">Sizes</div>
      <div class="doc-row">
        {(['xs', 'sm', 'md', 'lg'] as const).map(size => (
          <Button key={size} variant="primary" size={size}>
            {size}
          </Button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div class="doc-sub">Icon, states, groups</div>
      <div class="doc-row">
        <Tooltip content="Copy">
          <IconButton icon={Copy} label="Copy" />
        </Tooltip>
        <IconButton icon={Settings2} label="Settings" variant="secondary" size="md" />
        <Button
          variant="primary"
          loading={saving}
          onClick={() => {
            setSaving(true);
            setTimeout(() => setSaving(false), 1400);
          }}
        >
          Save changes
        </Button>
        <Button loading>Loading</Button>
        <Button variant="primary" disabled>
          Disabled
        </Button>
        <ButtonGroup label="Alignment">
          <IconButton icon={AlignLeft} label="Align left" variant="secondary" />
          <IconButton icon={AlignCenter} label="Align center" variant="secondary" />
          <IconButton icon={AlignRight} label="Align right" variant="secondary" />
        </ButtonGroup>
        <ButtonGroup label="Create">
          <Button size="sm" icon={Plus}>
            Create
          </Button>
          <IconButton icon={ChevronDown} label="More create options" variant="secondary" />
        </ButtonGroup>
      </div>
      <div class="doc-sub">Links</div>
      <div class="doc-row">
        <a class="ui-link" href="#buttons">
          Inline link
        </a>
        <a class="ui-link" href="#buttons">
          Documentation <ArrowUpRight class="ui-icon-sm" aria-hidden="true" />
        </a>
        <a class="ui-link--muted" href="#buttons">
          Muted link
        </a>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

type Model = 'planner' | 'executor' | 'vision';

export function InputsSection() {
  const toast = useToast();
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [model, setModel] = useState<Model | undefined>('planner');
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState('');
  const [tasks, setTasks] = useState(3);
  const [code, setCode] = useState('');
  const [senders, setSenders] = useState(['team@acme.com', 'ops@acme.com']);
  const [shortcut, setShortcut] = useState(['⌘', '⇧', 'O']);
  return (
    <section class="doc-section" id="inputs">
      <h2>Inputs</h2>
      <p>40px fields with an 8px radius. Labels above, hints and errors below; the invalid state is aria-invalid.</p>
      <div class="doc-grid doc-grid--2">
        <div class="doc-col">
          <Field label="Full name">
            <Input value="Denis" />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Occupation" optional hint="Used to personalize responses across tasks.">
            <Input placeholder="Product designer" />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Email" error="Enter a valid email address">
            <Input value="not-an-email" />
          </Field>
          <Field label="User ID">
            <Input value="310519664054860552" readOnly action={<IconButton icon={Copy} label="Copy user ID" />} />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Disabled">
            <Input value="Can't touch this" disabled />
          </Field>
        </div>
        <div class="doc-col">
          <Field label="Search">
            <Input icon={Search} placeholder="Search tasks" kbd="/" />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Password">
            <Input
              type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
              value="hunter22"
              action={
                <IconButton
                  icon={showPassword ? EyeOff : Eye}
                  label={showPassword ? 'Hide password' : 'Show password'}
                  onClick={() => setShowPassword(s => !s)}
                />
              }
            />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Website">
            <Input prefix="https://" suffix=".agent.app" placeholder="your-site" />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Language">
            <NativeSelect
              options={[
                { value: 'en', label: 'English' },
                { value: 'de', label: 'Deutsch' },
              ]}
            />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Default model">
            <Select
              icon={Cpu}
              value={model}
              onChange={setModel}
              options={[
                { value: 'planner', label: 'Planner · DeepSeek', description: 'Best plans' },
                { value: 'executor', label: 'Executor · Qwen', description: 'Fast' },
                { value: 'vision', label: 'Vision model', description: <Badge>Soon</Badge>, disabled: true },
              ]}
            />
          </Field>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="doc-sub">Textarea with counter</div>
      <Field label="Custom instructions" hint="Applied to every new task." counter={`${notes.length} / 300`}>
        <Textarea
          autosize
          maxLength={300}
          placeholder="Be concise. Default to Python unless I say otherwise."
          value={notes}
          onInput={e => setNotes(e.currentTarget.value)}
        />
      </Field>
      <div class="doc-sub">Specialized</div>
      <div class="doc-grid doc-grid--2">
        <div class="doc-col">
          <Field label="Concurrent tasks">
            <Stepper value={tasks} min={1} max={20} onChange={setTasks} />
          </Field>
          <OtpInput
            label="Verification code"
            value={code}
            onChange={setCode}
            onComplete={c => toast.show({ tone: 'success', title: `Code ${c} accepted` })}
          />
          <Field label="Approved senders">
            <TagInput
              values={senders}
              onChange={setSenders}
              placeholder="Add email, press Enter"
              validate={v => v.includes('@')}
            />
          </Field>
        </div>
        <div class="doc-col">
          <Dropzone
            hint="CSV, XLSX, PDF, images · up to 50 MB"
            onFiles={files => toast.show({ tone: 'info', title: `${files.length} file(s) ready to upload` })}
          />
          <ShortcutInput label="New task shortcut" value={shortcut} onChange={setShortcut} />
          <div>
            Agent's email:{' '}
            <span
              class="ui-inline-edit"
              contentEditable
              spellcheck={false}
              role="textbox"
              tabIndex={0}
              aria-label="Agent email"
            >
              denis-1067@agent.bot
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

type Busy = 'send' | 'queue' | 'interrupt';

export function SelectionSection() {
  const { theme, setTheme } = useTheme();
  const [notify, setNotify] = useState(true);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<Busy>('send');
  const [updates, setUpdates] = useState(true);
  const [cap, setCap] = useState(6000);
  const [billing, setBilling] = useState<'monthly' | 'yearly'>('monthly');
  const [view, setView] = useState<'list' | 'grid'>('grid');
  return (
    <section class="doc-section" id="selection">
      <h2>Selection</h2>
      <p>Native inputs styled with appearance: none — keyboard and screen readers work for free.</p>
      <div class="doc-grid">
        <div class="doc-col">
          <Checkbox label="Browser notifications" checked={notify} onChange={e => setNotify(e.currentTarget.checked)} />
          <Checkbox label="Select all tasks" indeterminate />
          <Checkbox label="Sound alert" description="Play a sound once when a task is completed." />
          <Checkbox label="Disabled" disabled />
        </div>
        <RadioGroup
          label="While a task is running"
          name="busy"
          value={busy}
          onChange={setBusy}
          options={[
            { value: 'send', label: 'Send without interrupting' },
            { value: 'queue', label: 'Queue the message' },
            {
              value: 'interrupt',
              label: 'Interrupt the task',
              description: 'The agent stops and reads your message first.',
            },
          ]}
        />
        <div class="doc-col">
          <Switch label="Product updates" checked={updates} onCheckedChange={setUpdates} />
          <Switch label="Compact" size="sm" checked onCheckedChange={() => {}} />
          <Switch label="Disabled" checked={false} disabled onCheckedChange={() => {}} />
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="doc-sub">Range</div>
      <Field label="Monthly credit cap" hint={`${cap.toLocaleString('en-US')} credits`}>
        <Slider
          min={1000}
          max={12000}
          step={500}
          value={cap}
          onValueChange={setCap}
          formatValue={v => `${v.toLocaleString('en-US')} credits`}
        />
      </Field>
      <div class="doc-sub">Segmented control</div>
      <div class="doc-row">
        <SegmentedControl
          label="Billing period"
          value={billing}
          onChange={setBilling}
          options={[
            { value: 'monthly', label: 'Monthly' },
            { value: 'yearly', label: 'Yearly · save 20%' },
          ]}
        />
        <SegmentedControl
          label="View"
          value={view}
          onChange={setView}
          options={[
            { value: 'list', label: 'List', icon: List },
            { value: 'grid', label: 'Grid', icon: LayoutGrid },
          ]}
        />
      </div>
      <div class="doc-sub">Choice cards</div>
      <ChoiceGroup
        label="Theme"
        name="theme-choice"
        value={theme}
        onChange={setTheme}
        options={[
          { value: 'light', label: 'Light', icon: Sun },
          { value: 'dark', label: 'Dark', icon: Moon },
          { value: 'auto', label: 'Auto', icon: SunMoon },
        ]}
      />
    </section>
  );
}

            Automations

            </span>
          </a>
          <div class="ui-nav-label">Tasks</div>
          <TaskItem title="Compare agent frameworks" status="running" href="#navigation" />
          <TaskItem
            title="Q3 supplier shortlist"
            status="done"
            href="#navigation"
            current
            menu={<IconButton icon={Ellipsis} label="Task menu" size="xs" />}
          />
          <TaskItem title="Scrape pricing pages" status="failed" href="#navigation" />
          <TaskItem title="Pick a supplier" status="waiting" href="#navigation" />
        </nav>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import {
  Archive,
  ChevronRight,
  CircleCheck,
  Command,
  Copy,
  Ellipsis,
  FileText,
  FolderInput,
  Gift,
  KeyRound,
  MonitorOff,
  Pencil,
  Pin,
  Plus,
  RotateCcw,
  Share,
  Sparkles,
  Trash2,
} from 'lucide-preact';
import {
  Alert,
  Banner,
  Button,
  Checkbox,
  ConfirmDialog,
  CreditBalance,
  Dialog,
  EmptyState,
  Field,
  Input,
  Menu,
  NativeSelect,
  Popover,
  Progress,
  Sheet,
  Skeleton,
  Spinner,
  StatusDot,
  Tooltip,
  useToast,
  type MenuEntry,
  type StatusTone,
} from '../../../src/index.ts';

const TOASTS: Record<StatusTone, [string, string]> = {
  success: ['Task completed', '3 files saved to your library.'],
  info: ['Agent is working', 'We’ll notify you when it’s done.'],
  warning: ['Credits running low', '96 credits left this month.'],
  danger: ['Couldn’t start sandbox', 'Please try again in a minute.'],
};

export function OverlaysSection({ onOpenPalette }: { onOpenPalette(): void }) {
  const toast = useToast();
  const [pinned, setPinned] = useState(true);
  const [dialog, setDialog] = useState(false);
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState(false);
  const [sheet, setSheet] = useState(false);
  const menu: MenuEntry[] = [
    { kind: 'label', id: 'task', label: 'Task' },
    {
      kind: 'item',
      id: 'copy',
      label: 'Copy',
      icon: Copy,
      trail: <ChevronRight class="ui-icon-sm" aria-hidden="true" />,
      onSelect: () => {},
    },
    { kind: 'item', id: 'rename', label: 'Rename', icon: Pencil, shortcut: ['R'], onSelect: () => {} },
    { kind: 'checkbox', id: 'pin', label: 'Pinned', icon: Pin, checked: pinned, onCheckedChange: setPinned },
    { kind: 'separator', id: 'sep1' },
    { kind: 'item', id: 'move', label: 'Move to project', icon: FolderInput, onSelect: () => {} },
    { kind: 'item', id: 'archive', label: 'Archive', icon: Archive, onSelect: () => {} },
    { kind: 'item', id: 'share', label: 'Share (owner only)', icon: Share, disabled: true, onSelect: () => {} },
    { kind: 'separator', id: 'sep2' },
    {
      kind: 'item',
      id: 'delete',
      label: 'Delete',
      icon: Trash2,
      danger: true,
      shortcut: ['⌘', '⌫'],
      onSelect: () => setConfirm(true),
    },
  ];
  return (
    <section class="doc-section" id="overlays">
      <h2>Overlays</h2>
      <p>
        Menus and popovers scale in from their anchor, dialogs rise and settle, sheets slide in. All close on Escape and
        outside click.
      </p>
      <div class="doc-row">
        <Menu
          items={menu}
          width={240}
          trigger={props => (
            <Button {...props} icon={Ellipsis}>
              Dropdown menu
            </Button>
          )}
        />
        <Popover
          label="Credits"
          trigger={props => (
            <Button {...props} icon={Sparkles}>
              Popover
            </Button>
          )}
        >
          <CreditBalance value={996} total={1300} hint="300 refresh credits every day at 00:00." />
          <Button variant="primary" size="sm" block>
            Get more credits
          </Button>
        </Popover>
        <Tooltip content="Tooltips appear after 400ms">
          <Button>Tooltip</Button>
        </Tooltip>
        <Button onClick={() => setDialog(true)}>Dialog</Button>
        <Button variant="danger-outline" onClick={() => setConfirm(true)}>
          Confirm dialog
        </Button>
        <Button onClick={() => setSheet(true)}>Sheet</Button>
        <Button icon={Command} onClick={onOpenPalette}>
          Command palette
        </Button>
      </div>
      <div class="doc-sub">Toasts</div>
      <div class="doc-row">
        {(Object.keys(TOASTS) as StatusTone[]).map(tone => (
          <Button
            key={tone}
            size="sm"
            onClick={() => toast.show({ tone, title: TOASTS[tone][0], description: TOASTS[tone][1] })}
          >
            {tone}
          </Button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <Dialog
        open={dialog}
        onOpenChange={setDialog}
        title="Create API key"
        description="Keys let your code start tasks and read results."
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setDialog(false)}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button
              variant="primary"
              onClick={() => {
                setDialog(false);
                toast.show({
                  tone: 'success',
                  title: 'API key created',
                  description: 'Copy it now — you won’t see it again.',
                });
              }}
            >
              Create key
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <Field label="Name">
          <Input placeholder="Production backend" />
        </Field>
        <Field label="Expires">
          <NativeSelect
            options={[
              { value: '30', label: 'In 30 days' },
              { value: '90', label: 'In 90 days' },
              { value: 'never', label: 'Never' },
            ]}
          />
        </Field>
        <Checkbox label="Allow starting tasks" description="Without this the key is read-only." checked />
      </Dialog>
      <ConfirmDialog
        open={confirm}
        onOpenChange={setConfirm}
        title="Delete this task?"
        description="The conversation and its files will be deleted. This can't be undone."
        confirmLabel="Delete task"
        onConfirm={() => {
          setConfirm(false);
          toast.show({
            tone: 'danger',
            title: 'Task deleted',
            description: 'You can restore it from Archive for 30 days.',
          });
        }}
      />
      <Sheet
        open={sheet}
        onOpenChange={setSheet}
        title="Task details"
        footer={
          <Button variant="primary" onClick={() => setSheet(false)}>
            Save
          </Button>
        }
      >
        <dl class="ui-dl">
          <dt>Status</dt>
          <dd>Completed</dd>
          <dt>Duration</dt>
          <dd>1m 27s</dd>
        </dl>
        <hr class="ui-divider" />
        <Field label="Note">
          <Input placeholder="Add a private note" />
        </Field>
      </Sheet>
    </section>
  );
}

export function FeedbackSection() {
  return (
    <section class="doc-section" id="feedback">
      <h2>Feedback</h2>
      <p>
        Inline alerts for context, toasts for results, progress for anything longer than a second, skeletons for
        content.
      </p>
      <div class="doc-col">
        <Alert
          icon={FileText}
          actions={
            <a class="ui-link--muted ui-text-sm" href="#feedback">
              Documentation ↗
            </a>
          }
        >
          Manage API keys to access the Agent API.
        </Alert>
        <Alert tone="info" title="Your agent is working in the background">
          You can close this tab — we'll notify you when the task is done.
        </Alert>
        <Alert tone="success" title="Task completed" actions={<Button size="sm">Open files</Button>}>
          3 files were saved to your library.
        </Alert>
        <Alert tone="warning" title="Partial result">
          The task hit its token budget. Here's what the agent finished.
        </Alert>
        <Alert
          tone="danger"
          title="Sandbox failed to start"
          actions={
            <Button size="sm" icon={RotateCcw}>
              Retry

        >

        <Banner icon={Gift}>
          Invite a friend and you both get 2,500 credits.{' '}
          <a class="ui-link" href="#feedback">
            Invite
          </a>
        </Banner>
      </div>
      <div class="doc-grid" style={{ marginTop: 'var(--space-6)' }}>
        <div class="ui-card doc-col">
          <b>Progress</b>
          <Progress label="Upload" value={72} />
          <Progress label="Done" value={100} tone="success" />
          <Progress label="Budget" value={91} tone="warning" size="sm" />
          <Progress label="Loading" indeterminate size="sm" />
        </div>
        <div class="ui-card">
          <CreditBalance value={996} total={1300} hint="Refreshes to 300 at 00:00 every day" />
        </div>
        <div class="ui-card doc-col">
          <b>Spinners &amp; status</b>
          <div class="doc-row">
            <Spinner size="sm" />
            <Spinner />
            <Spinner size="lg" tone="accent" label="Loading" />
          </div>
          <div class="doc-row ui-text-sm">
            {(
              [
                ['success', 'Online'],
                ['info', 'Working'],
                ['warning', 'Waiting'],
                ['danger', 'Failed'],
                ['neutral', 'Idle'],
              ] as const
            ).map(([tone, text]) => (
              <span key={tone} class="doc-row" style={{ gap: '6px' }}>
                <StatusDot tone={tone} pulse={tone === 'info'} />
                {text}
              </span>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="ui-card doc-col">
          <b>Skeleton</b>
          <div class="doc-row" style={{ flexWrap: 'nowrap' }}>
            <Skeleton variant="circle" width={32} height={32} />
            <div style={{ flex: 1, display: 'grid', gap: '8px' }}>
              <Skeleton variant="text" width="60%" />
              <Skeleton variant="text" width="90%" />
            </div>
          </div>
          <Skeleton height={72} />
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="doc-sub">Empty states</div>
      <div class="doc-grid doc-grid--2">
        <div class="ui-card">
          <EmptyState
            icon={KeyRound}
            title="No API keys yet"
            description="Use API keys to authenticate and start tasks from your own code."
            actions={
              <Button size="sm" icon={Plus}>
                Create key
              </Button>
            }
          />
        </div>
        <div class="ui-card">
          <EmptyState
            icon={MonitorOff}
            title="No cloud computers"
            description="Create an always-on workspace so your agent can work 24/7."
            actions={
              <>
                <Button variant="primary" size="sm">
                  Create now
                </Button>
                <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" icon={CircleCheck}>
                  Learn more
                </Button>
              </>
            }
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```
