```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { Check, Cloud, Copy, Folder, Mic, Plus, RotateCcw, SlidersHorizontal } from 'lucide-preact';
import {
  AgentMessage,
  AgentText,
  AskCard,
  Attachment,
  Badge,
  Button,
  Chip,
  Composer,
  ComputerPanel,
  FileCard,
  IconButton,
  PlanList,
  Step,
  StepGroup,
  Suggestion,
  TaskItem,
  Thinking,
  UserMessage,
  useToast,
  type PlanStatus,
} from '../../../src/index.ts';

export function AgentSection() {
  const toast = useToast();
  const [task, setTask] = useState('');
  const [files, setFiles] = useState(['suppliers_2026.xlsx']);
  return (
    <section class="doc-section" id="agent">
      <h2>Agent</h2>
      <p>The composer is the hero of every screen. Questions from the agent stand out in the accent.</p>
      <div class="doc-sub">Composer</div>
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 'var(--content-width)' }}>
        <Composer
          value={task}
          onValueChange={setTask}
          onSubmit={value => {
            toast.show({ tone: 'info', title: 'Task sent', description: value });
            setTask('');
          }}
          placeholder="Give your agent a task, or type / for commands"
          context={
            <>
              <Chip ghost icon={Cloud}>
                Cloud
              </Chip>
              <Chip ghost icon={Folder}>
                Choose project
              </Chip>
            </>
          }
          attachments={
            files.length > 0 &&
            files.map(name => (
              <Attachment
                key={name}
                name={name}
                meta="Spreadsheet · 84 KB"
                onRemove={() => setFiles(list => list.filter(f => f !== name))}
              />
            ))
          }
          tools={
            <>
              <IconButton icon={Plus} label="Attach files" />
              <IconButton icon={SlidersHorizontal} label="Tools" />
            </>
          }
          actions={<IconButton icon={Mic} label="Voice input" />}
        />
      </div>
      <div class="doc-grid doc-grid--2" style={{ marginTop: 'var(--space-8)' }}>
        <div class="doc-col">
          <div class="doc-sub">Agent asks the user · ask_user</div>
          <AskCard
            question="Should I include closed-source frameworks with a public GitHub repo, or only fully open-source ones?"
            options={['Only open-source', 'Include both']}
            onAnswer={answer => toast.show({ tone: 'success', title: 'Answer sent', description: answer })}
          />
        </div>
        <div class="doc-col">
          <div class="doc-sub">Results &amp; next steps</div>
          <div class="doc-row">
            <FileCard name="agent_frameworks.csv" meta="Spreadsheet · 128 B" href="#agent" />
            <FileCard name="research_notes.md" meta="Document · 4 KB" href="#agent" />
            <FileCard name="summary_deck.pptx" meta="Slides · 1.2 MB" href="#agent" />
            <FileCard name="fetch_stars.py" meta="Python · 2 KB" onOpen={() => {}} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <Suggestion onSelect={() => setTask('Visualize stars in a bar chart')}>
              Visualize stars in a bar chart
            </Suggestion>
            <Suggestion onSelect={() => setTask('Compare their architectures')}>Compare their architectures</Suggestion>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

const PLAN = ['Find candidate frameworks', 'Collect stars, language and license', 'Write the CSV'];
const SCRIPT = [
  {
    sub: 0,
    tool: 'web_search',
    text: 'Searching "most starred open-source agent frameworks"',
    activity: 'Using Search',
    detail: 'agent framework github stars',
    body: '# Search results\n1. LangChain\n2. Browser Use\n3. AutoGen',
  },
  {
    sub: 0,
    tool: 'browser_open',
    text: 'Reading a ranking of agent repositories',
    activity: 'Using Browser',
    detail: 'github.com/topics/ai-agents',
    body: '# Topic: ai-agents\n[1] langchain-ai/langchain\n[2] browser-use/browser-use',
  },
  {
    sub: 1,
    tool: 'shell',
    text: 'Fetching repository metadata from the GitHub API',
    activity: 'Using Terminal',
    detail: '~/workspace',
    body: '$ curl -s api.github.com/repos/browser-use/browser-use | jq .stargazers_count\n119617',
  },
  {
    sub: 2,
    tool: 'file_write',
    text: 'Writing outputs/agent_frameworks.csv',
    activity: 'Using Editor',
    detail: 'outputs/agent_frameworks.csv',
    body: 'framework,stars,license\nLangChain,148752,MIT\nBrowser Use,119617,MIT',
  },
] as const;
const STEP_MS = 1500;

/** Экран задачи: прогон агента, проигрываемый по шагам (сайдбар, ответ, план, шаги, компьютер). */
export function TaskScreenSection() {
  const [run, setRun] = useState(0);
  const [step, setStep] = useState(-1);
  const [stopped, setStopped] = useState(false);
  const [elapsed, setElapsed] = useState(0);
  const [stepsOpen, setStepsOpen] = useState(true);
  const done = step >= SCRIPT.length;
  const working = !done && !stopped;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!working) return;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setStep(s => s + 1), step < 0 ? 900 : STEP_MS);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [step, working, run]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!working) return;
    const tick = setInterval(() => setElapsed(s => s + 1), 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(tick);
  }, [working, run]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (done) setStepsOpen(false);
  }, [done]);

  const replay = () => {
    setRun(r => r + 1);
    setStep(-1);
    setElapsed(0);
    setStopped(false);
    setStepsOpen(true);
  };
  const current = SCRIPT[Math.min(step, SCRIPT.length - 1)];
  const status = (index: number): PlanStatus => {
    if (done) return 'done';
    const sub = current && step >= 0 ? current.sub : -1;
    if (index < sub) return 'done';
    if (index === sub) return stopped ? 'failed' : 'running';
    return 'pending';
  };
  const shown = SCRIPT.slice(0, Math.max(0, Math.min(step + 1, SCRIPT.length)));

  return (
    <section class="doc-section" id="task">
      <h2>Task screen</h2>
      <p>
        Planner and executors made visible: the plan, a collapsible step log with a live shimmer, and the agent's
        computer.
      </p>
      <div class="doc-row" style={{ marginBottom: 'var(--space-3)' }}>
        <Button size="sm" icon={RotateCcw} onClick={replay}>
          Replay run
        </Button>
      </div>
      <div class="shell">
        <aside class="shell-side">
          <nav class="ui-nav" aria-label="Tasks">
            <div class="ui-nav-label">Tasks</div>
            <TaskItem
              title="Compare agent frameworks"
              status={done ? 'done' : stopped ? 'idle' : 'running'}
              href="#task"
              current
            />
            <TaskItem title="Q3 supplier shortlist" status="idle" href="#task" />
          </nav>
        </aside>
        <div class="shell-chat">
          <div class="shell-body">
            <UserMessage>
              Find the 3 most popular open-source agent frameworks on GitHub and save a comparison as CSV.
            </UserMessage>
            <AgentMessage
              working={working}
              elapsed={elapsed}
              mark={<span class="mark mark--sm" />}
              badge={<Badge variant="outline">Beta</Badge>}
              onShowDetails={() => setStepsOpen(true)}
            >
              <AgentText>Here's my plan:</AgentText>
              <PlanList items={PLAN.map((title, index) => ({ id: String(index), title, status: status(index) }))} />
              {shown.length > 0 && (
                <StepGroup
                  summary={
                    done ? 'Searched 1 query · Browsed 1 page · Ran 1 command · Wrote 1 file' : `${shown.length} steps`
                  }
                  expanded={stepsOpen}
                  onExpandedChange={setStepsOpen}
                >
                  {shown.map((s, index) => (
                    <Step key={s.text} tool={s.tool} active={working && index === shown.length - 1}>
                      {s.text}
                    </Step>
                  ))}
                </StepGroup>
              )}
              {working && <Thinking />}
              {done && (
                <>
                  <AgentText>LangChain, Browser Use and Pi lead by stars; all three are MIT-licensed.</AgentText>
                  <FileCard name="agent_frameworks.csv" meta="Spreadsheet · 128 B" href="#task" />
                  <div class="ui-agent__actions">
                    <Badge tone="success" icon={Check}>
                      Task completed
                    </Badge>
                    <span class="ui-spacer" />
                    <IconButton icon={Copy} label="Copy answer" size="xs" />
                    <IconButton icon={RotateCcw} label="Retry" size="xs" onClick={replay} />
                  </div>
                </>
              )}
            </AgentMessage>
          </div>
          <div class="shell-foot">
            <Composer
              value=""
              onValueChange={() => {}}
              onSubmit={() => {}}
              running={working}
              onStop={() => setStopped(true)}
              placeholder="Message your agent"
            />
            <small>Agents can make mistakes. Check important results.</small>
          </div>
        </div>
        <ComputerPanel
          activity={current && step >= 0 ? current.activity : 'Starting'}
          detail={current && step >= 0 ? current.detail : 'sandbox'}
          position={(Math.min(step + 1, SCRIPT.length) / SCRIPT.length) * 100}
          live
        >
          {current && step >= 0 ? current.body : ''}
        </ComputerPanel>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```
`packages/ui/showcase/src/App.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import {
  Activity,
  Bell,
  Bot,
  Layers,
  Monitor,
  Moon,
  MousePointerClick,
  Navigation,
  Palette,
  PanelsTopLeft,
  Ruler,
  Search,
  Shapes,
  SquarePen,
  Sun,
  Table2,
  TextCursorInput,
  ToggleRight,
  Type,
  type LucideIcon,
} from 'lucide-preact';
import {
  Badge,
  Button,
  CommandPalette,
  KbdCombo,
  SegmentedControl,
  ToastProvider,
  useHotkey,
  useTheme,
  type CommandGroup,
} from '../../src/index.ts';
import { AgentSection, TaskScreenSection } from './sections/agent.tsx';
import { AuthSection } from './sections/auth.tsx';
import { ButtonsSection, InputsSection, NavigationSection, SelectionSection } from './sections/controls.tsx';
import { DataSection } from './sections/data.tsx';
import { ColorSection, FoundationsSection } from './sections/foundations.tsx';
import { FeedbackSection, OverlaysSection } from './sections/overlays.tsx';

export const NAV: Array<[string, Array<[string, string, LucideIcon]>]> = [
  [
    'Foundations',
    [
      ['color', 'Color', Palette],
      ['type', 'Typography', Type],
      ['layout', 'Space, radius, depth', Ruler],
      ['motion', 'Motion', Activity],
      ['icons', 'Icons', Shapes],
    ],
  ],
  [
    'Components',
    [
      ['buttons', 'Buttons', MousePointerClick],
      ['inputs', 'Inputs', TextCursorInput],
      ['selection', 'Selection', ToggleRight],
      ['navigation', 'Navigation', Navigation],
      ['overlays', 'Overlays', Layers],
      ['feedback', 'Feedback', Bell],
      ['data', 'Data display', Table2],
    ],
  ],
  [
    'Patterns',
    [
      ['agent', 'Agent', Bot],
      ['task', 'Task screen', PanelsTopLeft],
    ],
  ],
];

function jumpTo(id: string) {
  document.getElementById(id)?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
}

function Showcase() {
  const { theme, setTheme } = useTheme();
  const [palette, setPalette] = useState(false);
  useHotkey('k', () => setPalette(true), { mod: true });
  const commands: CommandGroup[] = [
    {
      label: 'Sections',
      items: NAV.flatMap(([, items]) =>
        items.map(([id, label, icon]) => ({ id, label, icon, onSelect: () => jumpTo(id) })),
      ),
    },
    {
      label: 'Theme',
      items: [
        { id: 'light', label: 'Light theme', icon: Sun, onSelect: () => setTheme('light') },
        { id: 'dark', label: 'Dark theme', icon: Moon, onSelect: () => setTheme('dark') },
        { id: 'auto', label: 'System theme', icon: Monitor, keywords: 'auto', onSelect: () => setTheme('auto') },
      ],
    },
    {
      label: 'Actions',
      items: [{ id: 'new', label: 'New task', icon: SquarePen, shortcut: ['⌘', 'O'], onSelect: () => jumpTo('agent') }],
    },
  ];
  return (
    <>
      <AuthSection />
      <header class="doc-top">
        <span class="mark mark--sm" />
        <span class="word">agent</span>
        <Badge variant="outline">Design system</Badge>
        <span class="ui-spacer" />
        <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" icon={Search} onClick={() => setPalette(true)}>
          Search <KbdCombo keys={['⌘', 'K']} />
        </Button>
        <SegmentedControl
          label="Theme"
          value={theme}
          onChange={setTheme}
          options={[
            { value: 'light', icon: Sun, ariaLabel: 'Light theme' },
            { value: 'dark', icon: Moon, ariaLabel: 'Dark theme' },
            { value: 'auto', icon: Monitor, ariaLabel: 'System theme' },
          ]}
        />
      </header>
      <div class="doc">
        <nav class="doc-nav ui-nav" aria-label="Sections">
          {NAV.map(([group, items]) => (
            <div key={group}>
              <div class="ui-nav-label">{group}</div>
              {items.map(([id, label, Icon]) => (
                <a key={id} class="ui-nav-item" href={`#${id}`}>
                  <Icon aria-hidden="true" />
                  {label}
                </a>
              ))}
            </div>
          ))}
        </nav>
        <main class="doc-main">
          <ColorSection />
          <FoundationsSection />
          <ButtonsSection />
          <InputsSection />
          <SelectionSection />
          <NavigationSection />
          <OverlaysSection onOpenPalette={() => setPalette(true)} />
          <FeedbackSection />
          <DataSection />
          <AgentSection />
          <TaskScreenSection />
        </main>
      </div>
      <CommandPalette open={palette} onOpenChange={setPalette} groups={commands} />
    </>
  );
}

/** Витрина ui-kit — живой эталон вместо design/preview.html. */
export function App() {
  return (
    <ToastProvider>
      <Showcase />
    </ToastProvider>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: README пакета и корня**

`packages/ui/README.md`:
````markdown
# @agent/ui

The design system as a package: tokens, `ui-*` CSS components, Preact components with behaviour, agent patterns and
the sign-in background. Rules and the component catalogue: [`docs/design/design-system.md`](../../docs/design/design-system.md).

## Use it in an app

```ts
import '@agent/ui/fonts.css'; // Geist, Geist Mono, Instrument Serif — self-hosted
import '@agent/ui/styles.css'; // tokens + every ui-* component
import { Button, ToastProvider, useTheme } from '@agent/ui';
```

- Put `class="ui-root"` on `<body>`: base font, focus ring, selection colour and the reduced-motion switch hang off it.
- Use only the kit for anything it covers. Need a new variant? Add it here, not in the app.
- Colours come from semantic tokens only (`--bg-surface`, `--text-secondary`, …). `test/styles.test.ts` fails on
  hard-coded colours and on `ui-*` classes that no stylesheet defines.

## Themes

`tokens.css` is dark by default. `data-theme` on `<html>` switches it: `light`, `dark` or `auto` (follows
`prefers-color-scheme`).

- `useTheme()` returns `{ theme, resolved, setTheme }`; every component that calls it sees the same theme, and the
  choice is saved in `localStorage` (`ui-theme`).
- Put `<script>${themeBootScript}</script>` first in `<head>` so the saved theme applies before the first paint
  (the showcase injects it from `vite.config.ts`). With a CSP, allow it by hash.

## What is inside

| Group             | Components                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ----------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Actions           | `Button`, `IconButton`, `ButtonGroup`, `Chip`, `Tag`                                                                                                                                                 |
| Fields            | `Field`, `Input`, `Textarea`, `NativeSelect`, `Select`, `Stepper`, `OtpInput`, `TagInput`, `Dropzone`, `ShortcutInput`                                                                               |
| Choice            | `Checkbox`, `Switch`, `RadioGroup`, `Slider`, `SegmentedControl`, `ChoiceGroup`                                                                                                                      |
| Navigation        | `Tabs`                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Overlays          | `Menu`, `Popover`, `Tooltip`, `Dialog`, `ConfirmDialog`, `Sheet`, `CommandPalette`, `ToastProvider` + `useToast`                                                                                     |
| Feedback and data | `Alert`, `Banner`, `Progress`, `Spinner`, `Skeleton`, `StatusDot`, `EmptyState`, `Badge`, `Avatar`, `AvatarGroup`, `Kbd`, `KbdCombo`                                                                 |
| Agent             | `Composer`, `Attachment`, `UserMessage`, `AgentMessage`, `AgentText`, `Thinking`, `AskCard`, `FileCard`, `Suggestion`, `PlanList`, `StepGroup`, `Step`, `TaskItem`, `ComputerPanel`, `CreditBalance` |
| Effects           | `DotField` (`mountDotField` for plain DOM)                                                                                                                                                           |
| Hooks             | `useTheme`, `useHotkey`, `useFloating`, `useDismiss`, `useFocusTrap`, `useScrollLock`, `usePresence`, `useReducedMotion`                                                                             |

Purely visual pieces stay CSS classes used as markup: cards, lists and rows, tables, description lists, dividers,
code blocks, accordions, breadcrumbs, pagination and side navigation. The showcase shows each of them.

## Showcase

```bash
bun run ui:dev     # http://localhost:5174 — every component, theme switcher, ⌘K palette
bun run ui:build   # static build in packages/ui/showcase/dist
```

## Tests

`bun run test:ui` runs the kit's tests in their own process with happy-dom preloaded (`test/dom.ts`): happy-dom
replaces `fetch`, `Request` and `Response`, so it must not share a process with the backend tests. Plain `bun test`
skips `packages/ui` (`pathIgnorePatterns` in `bunfig.toml`); `bun run test` and `bun run test:unit` call `test:ui`
after the backend tests. Test behaviour (keyboard, focus, ARIA state), not markup snapshots. Wait for removal with
`gone()` from `test/helpers.ts`: a failing `expect()` inside `waitFor` is slow in Bun.
````
В корневом `README.md` перед `## Docker stack`:
````markdown
## UI kit

```bash
bun run ui:dev              # component showcase on http://localhost:5174
bun run ui:build            # static showcase build in packages/ui/showcase/dist
bun run test:ui             # ui-kit tests (happy-dom in its own process; part of test:unit)
```

`packages/ui` (`@agent/ui`) is the design system: tokens, `ui-*` styles, Preact components and agent patterns.
Usage, themes and the component list: `packages/ui/README.md`.

````

```markdown
**План 6 (веб-приложение), итоги плана 5:**
- Подключение: `"@agent/ui": "workspace:*"` в `apps/web`, в точке входа `import '@agent/ui/fonts.css'` и `import '@agent/ui/styles.css'`, `<body class="ui-root">`, `themeBootScript` первым в `<head>` (как плагин `theme-boot` в `packages/ui/showcase/vite.config.ts`). CSP: inline-скрипт темы — по хэшу (`script-src 'self' 'sha256-…'`), шрифты — со своего origin; стили, которые Floating UI и Preact ставят через `element.style`, `style-src` не ограничивает.
- Статусы ядра → интерфейс: подзадача `pending` → `pending`, `running` и `reviewing` → `running`, `accepted` → `done`, `failed` → `failed`, `skipped` → `skipped` (`PlanList`); задача `queued`, `planning`, `running`, `finalizing` → `running`, `waiting_user` → `waiting`, `done` → `done`, `failed` → `failed`, `cancelled` → `idle` (`TaskItem`). Шаг `tool_result` → `<Step tool={name}>`, вопрос `ask_user` → `AskCard`, файлы → `FileCard href="/api/files/:id"`.
- Тема из профиля (спецификация 12): при входе `setTheme(profile.theme)`, при смене в настройках — `setTheme` и сохранение в профиль; `localStorage['ui-theme']` — только кэш для первой отрисовки.
- Кредиты: `CreditBalance` с числом кредитов и `format`, который показывает строку `credits` из `GET /api/me` (`formatCredits`), — без потери точности.
- Спецификация 2 называет Tailwind для фронтенда, а ui-kit — обычный CSS. Решить в начале плана 6: раскладка экранов на Tailwind поверх токенов или свои классы `app-*`; дублировать то, что есть в ui-kit, нельзя в обоих случаях.
- `ComputerPanel` показывает текст как есть: Markdown снимка страницы приложение рендерит само (безопасно), «последний скриншот браузера» (спецификация 12) — картинка внутри панели.
- Дымовой тест экранов и витрины в браузере (Playwright) и визуальная регрессия обеих тем — план 6 или 7: happy-dom не проверяет раскладку, `:has()` и анимации.
- Preact 11 (`latest` — 11.1.1): решить до начала `apps/web`, переводить ui-kit и web вместе (`forwardRef` → `ref` как обычный пропс).
- Vite в монорепозитории с isolated installs запускается из каталога пакета (`bun run --cwd apps/web dev`), как `ui:dev`: в корневом `node_modules` его нет.
```

## Что дальше

План 6 (веб-приложение) собирает экраны из `@agent/ui`: вход с `DotField` и magic link, главный экран с `Composer` и списком `TaskItem`, экран задачи (`AgentMessage`, `PlanList`, `StepGroup`/`Step`, `AskCard`, `FileCard`, `ComputerPanel`, кнопка «стоп» композера), настройки (`Tabs`, строки `ui-row`, `Switch`, `ChoiceGroup` темы) и `CommandPalette` по ⌘K. Перевод статусов, тема из профиля, CSP, Tailwind и Preact 11 — заметки «План 6 (веб-приложение), итоги плана 5» в дорожной карте. Сервис `web` за nginx подключается так же, как `api` (заметки плана 4b).

Новые компоненты и варианты добавляются в ui-kit, а не в приложение: CSS в `styles/<группа>.css` на семантических токенах, компонент в `src/`, тест поведения в `test/`, пример на витрине (тест витрины требует каждый экспортированный компонент).
